<!-- ============================================================
     AUTHENTICATION STATE CHANGE HANDLER
     Clears bot-related cookies when user signs out/in
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Helper to delete cookie
    function deleteCookie(name) {
        document.cookie = name + '=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC; path=/;';
        console.log('[Auth State] Cleared cookie:', name);
    }

    // Helper to set cookie
    function setCookie(name, value, days) {
        const expires = days ? `; expires=${new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString()}` : '';
        document.cookie = `${name}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}${expires}; path=/`;
        console.log('[Auth State] Set cookie:', name, '=', value);
    }

    // Check if user authentication state changed (login/logout)
    var lastUserId = sessionStorage.getItem('apex__lastUserId');
    var currentUserId = null;

    // Try to get current user ID from Aura (after it loads)
    var checkAuthState = function() {
        try {
            // Check if $A (Aura framework) is available
            if (typeof $A !== 'undefined' && $A.get) {
                currentUserId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id');

                // If user ID changed (sign-out or different user signed in)
                // ✅ FIX: Detect ANY user change (guest→auth OR auth→guest)
                // ⚠️ RELOAD FIX: Only trigger if Aura has explicitly loaded and returned a value
                // Don't trigger if currentUserId is undefined (Aura not ready on page reload)
                // Valid states from Aura: string (user ID), '' (guest), or null (guest)
                // Invalid state: undefined (Aura hasn't loaded yet)
                var auraHasLoaded = (typeof currentUserId === 'string' || currentUserId === null);
                if (lastUserId !== currentUserId && lastUserId && auraHasLoaded) {
                    console.log('[Auth State] User changed from', lastUserId, 'to', currentUserId);
                    console.log('[Auth State] Clearing bot-related cookies');

                    // Clear variation and locale cookies
                    deleteCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
                    deleteCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');
                    deleteCookie('csp_user_locale');

                    // Clear bot state from storage
                    try {
                        localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
                        localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_VARIATION');
                        localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                        sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
                        sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                        console.log('[Auth State] Cleared bot storage including variation');
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.warn('[Auth State] Could not clear storage:', e);
                    }

                    // ✅ FIX: Re-evaluate bot variation for new user (guest or different authenticated user)
                    // Set a flag so the Aura component can detect this and re-run loadPortalCapacity
                    sessionStorage.setItem('apex__needsVariationRefresh', 'true');
                    console.log('[Auth State] Set flag to refresh bot variation for new user');

                    // ✅ IMMEDIATE FIX: Trigger Aura event to reload bot variation
                    // This ensures the cookie is re-set immediately without waiting for page reload
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try {
                            // Dispatch custom event for Aura component to handle
                            var event = new CustomEvent('authStateChanged', {
                                bubbles: true,
                                composed: true,
                                detail: { userId: currentUserId || 'guest' }
                            });
                            document.dispatchEvent(event);
                            console.log('[Auth State] Dispatched authStateChanged event');
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.warn('[Auth State] Could not dispatch event:', e);
                        }
                    }, 500);
                }

                // Update last user ID
                sessionStorage.setItem('apex__lastUserId', currentUserId || '');
            }
        } catch (e) {
            // Aura not ready yet, will retry
        }
    };

    // Check on page load (after short delay for Aura to initialize)
    setTimeout(checkAuthState, 1000);
    setTimeout(checkAuthState, 2000); // Retry after 2s in case Aura loads late
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     STEP 0: SET BODY CLASS (CRITICAL!)
     This MUST run before all other bot logic
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Get cookie value
    function getCookie(name) {
        const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
        return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
    }

    // Set cookie
    function setCookie(name, value, days) {
        const expires = days ? `; expires=${new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString()}` : '';
        document.cookie = `${name}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}${expires}; path=/`;
    }

    // Get current cookie value
    let portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

    // CRITICAL FIX: Don't set default cookie immediately
    // Let the Aura component (SupportSiteCustomThemeLayout) decide the correct variation
    // based on locale and capacity checks
    // If no cookie exists, use empty string as placeholder
    if (!portalSearchName) {
        portalSearchName = ''; // Placeholder - Aura will set correct value
    } 

    // Make value globally accessible IMMEDIATELY (before body exists)
    window.portalSearchVariation = portalSearchName;

    // Function to set body class
    function setBodyClass() {
        if (!document.body) {
            setTimeout(setBodyClass, 10);
            return;
        }

        // Remove existing variation classes and attributes
        document.body.classList.remove('agenticSearch', 'globalSearch');
        document.body.removeAttribute('data-portal-search');

        // Only add class and attribute if portalSearchName is set
        // If empty, Aura component will set it later
        if (portalSearchName) {
            document.body.classList.add(portalSearchName);
            document.body.setAttribute('data-portal-search', portalSearchName);
        }
    }

    // Set body class as soon as body is available
    if (document.body) {
        setBodyClass();
    } else if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setBodyClass);
    } else {
        setBodyClass();
    }

})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UNIFIED ARCHITECTURE: Overlay Prevention
     Both V1 and V2 use same agenticEmbeddedChatWindow component
     Server-side inbound flow handles V1/V2 routing
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Only run for agenticSearch variation
    if (!window.portalSearchVariation || window.portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    // Set overlay prevention flag for unified architecture
    window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ = true;
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UNIFIED: Agentic Chat Setup (Only for agenticSearch)
     No V1/V2 detection - server handles routing
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Helper to get cookie
    function getCookie(name) {
        const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
        return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
    }

    // Only run for agenticSearch
    const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
    if (!portalSearchVariation || portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    /**
     * Check if agentic component is active (home page)
     */
    window.isAgentforceV2Active = function() {
        const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
        if (portalSearchName !== 'agenticSearch') {
            return false;
        }

        const componentExists = document.querySelector('c-agentic-embedded-chat-window') !== null;
        if (!componentExists) {
            return false;
        }

        const getPageName = window.getPageName || function() {
            const urlSegments = location.pathname.split('/').filter(segment => segment.trim());
            return urlSegments.length >= 3 ? urlSegments[2] : 'home';
        };
        const currentPage = getPageName();
        return currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '';
    };

    // Setup chat flags
    function setupAgenticChat() {
        const conversationExists = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                 sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
        const conversationEnded = sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true';

        //  NEW SESSION DETECTION: Use a session marker to detect new browser sessions
        // If marker doesn't exist, this is a new browser session (sessionStorage was cleared)
        const sessionMarker = sessionStorage.getItem('BROWSER_SESSION_ACTIVE');
        const isNewBrowserSession = !sessionMarker;

        // Set marker for this session
        if (!sessionMarker) {
            sessionStorage.setItem('BROWSER_SESSION_ACTIVE', 'true');
        }

        //  FIX: If conversation ended, clear everything
        if (conversationEnded) {
            console.log('[setupAgenticChat] 🧹 Conversation ended - clearing all flags');
            localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
            localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
            localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
            localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
            return;
        }

        //  NEW SESSION FIX: If this is a new browser session (browser was closed/reopened)
        // Clear localStorage flag to prevent auto-launch from previous session
        if (isNewBrowserSession) {
            const flagInLocalStorage = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';
            if (flagInLocalStorage && !conversationExists) {
                console.log('[setupAgenticChat] 🧹 New session without conversation - clearing stale flag');
                localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
                return;
            }
        }

        //  FIX: Preserve/set flags if conversation exists or flags already set
        const flagInSession = sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';
        const flagInLocalStorage = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

        console.log('[setupAgenticChat] 🔍 Flag check:', {
            flagInSession: flagInSession,
            flagInLocalStorage: flagInLocalStorage,
            shouldSetFlags: (conversationExists || flagInSession || flagInLocalStorage)
        });

        if (conversationExists || flagInSession || flagInLocalStorage) {
            // Set both localStorage and sessionStorage
            localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
            sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
            console.log('[setupAgenticChat] ✅ Set AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE flags');
        } else {
            console.log('[setupAgenticChat] ⚠️ No conversation or flags - not setting AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
        }
    }

    // Setup immediately
    setupAgenticChat();

    // 🔧 RELOAD FIX: Re-check flags after SDK initializes
    // The SDK restores conversation ID from cookies AFTER bootstrap.init() completes
    // So we need to wait for that and then re-check AND trigger bot load
    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingReady', function() {
        console.log('[setupAgenticChat] 🔄 SDK ready - re-checking conversation state');
        setTimeout(function() {
            setupAgenticChat();

            // 🔧 RELOAD FIX: Also trigger bot load after SDK restores conversation
            console.log('[setupAgenticChat] 🚀 Triggering bot load after SDK ready');
            if (typeof window.loadV2BotGlobally === 'function') {
                window.loadV2BotGlobally();
            }
        }, 500); // Wait for SDK to fully restore state
    }, { once: true });
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UNIFIED: Agentic Chat Global Loading
     Only load when conversation exists (agenticSearch variation)
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Helper to get cookie
    function getCookie(name) {
        const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
        return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
    }

    // Check variation cookie
    const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
    if (!portalSearchVariation || portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    // Define pages where bot is allowed
    window.V2_BOT_ALLOWED_PAGES = [
        'home',
        'article',
        'case',
        'topic',
        'global-search',
        'contactsupport',
		'countries-and-phone'
    ];

    window.isV2BotAllowedOnCurrentPage = function() {
        const getPageName = window.getPageName || function() {
            const path = window.location.pathname;
            const segments = path.split('/').filter(s => s);
            return segments.length >= 3 ? segments[2] : 'home';
        };
        const currentPage = getPageName();

        return window.V2_BOT_ALLOWED_PAGES.some(allowedPage =>
            currentPage === allowedPage || currentPage.includes(allowedPage)
        );
    };

    /**
     * Load agentic chat globally across pages (when conversation exists)
     */
    window.loadV2BotGlobally = function() {

        // ✅ IOS LOGO CLICK FIX: Check for logo navigation cookie
        // If logo navigation just happened, reset initialization flag to allow fresh load
        const logoNavCookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(row => row.startsWith('apex__logoNav='));
        if (logoNavCookie) {
            const timestamp = parseInt(logoNavCookie.split('=')[1]);
            const age = Date.now() - timestamp;

            // If logo navigation happened within last 5 seconds, reset initialization flag
            if (age < 5000) {
                window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
            }
        }

        if (window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__) {
            return;
        }

        // CROSS-TAB FIX: Don't check portal search cookie - it might not be loaded yet in new tab
        // Cross-tab only called when conversation exists, so safe to proceed
        // Aura/LWC already set this cookie in Tab 1, will be set eventually in Tab 2
        const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

        // Only skip if cookie is explicitly 'globalSearch' (not just missing)
        if (portalSearchName === 'globalSearch') {
            return;
        }

        // CROSS-TAB FIX: Check if locale just changed IN THIS TAB
        // Use sessionStorage (tab-specific) not localStorage (browser-wide)
        // This prevents blocking cross-tab when locale changed in DIFFERENT tab
        const localeJustChanged = sessionStorage.getItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
        const changeTime = sessionStorage.getItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');

        if (localeJustChanged === 'true' && changeTime) {
            const changeAge = Date.now() - parseInt(changeTime);

            //  Sanity check: If changeAge is negative or too large, clear stale flags
            if (changeAge < 0 || changeAge > 300000) { // 5 minutes max
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');
            }
            // If changed within last 2 seconds IN THIS TAB, don't auto-launch
            else if (changeAge < 2000) {
                // Clear the flags
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');

                //  CROSS-TAB FIX: Only clear sessionStorage conversation ID (tab-specific)
                // Keep localStorage conversation ID so other tabs can still detect cross-tab
                const hasActiveConversation = sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';
                if (hasActiveConversation) {
                    sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                }
                return; //  EXIT - Don't auto-launch in THIS tab
            } else {
                // Flag is old, clear it
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
                sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');
            }
        }

        //  CROSS-TAB FIX: Simplified - just check conversation ID + not ended
        // Don't require AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE flag (might not be set in new tab)
        const hasConversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                 sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
        const conversationEnded = sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                 localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true';

        if (!hasConversationId) {
            return;
        }

        //  NEW: Don't auto-launch if conversation has ended
        if (conversationEnded) {
            // Clear all conversation-related IDs and flags
            localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
            localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
            localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
            return;
        }

        const isAllowed = window.isV2BotAllowedOnCurrentPage();
        if (!isAllowed) {
            return;
        }

        // If already loaded, just launch
        if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
            typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined') {

            // Set V2 flags to prevent overlay
            window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ = true;
            //  Set flag when loading for existing conversation (cross-tab scenario)
            localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
            sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');

            // CRITICAL: Remove fake-inline class ONLY if NOT on home page
            // At 80% zoom, this timing is critical - don't remove the class on home page reload
            const getPageName = window.getPageName || function() {
                const path = window.location.pathname;
                const segments = path.split('/').filter(s => s);
                return segments.length >= 3 ? segments[2] : 'home';
            };
            const currentPage = getPageName();
            const isHomePage = currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '';

            // Only remove fake-inline-chat if we're NOT on home page
            if (!isHomePage && document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
                console.log('[loadV2BotGlobally] Removed fake-inline-chat (not home page)');
            } else if (isHomePage) {
                console.log('[loadV2BotGlobally] Keeping fake-inline-chat (home page)');
            }

            const attemptLaunch = function() {
                const buttonExists = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton');

                if (buttonExists) {
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                            try {
                                window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                            } catch (e) {
                                console.error('[V2 Global] Launch error:', e);
                            }
                        }
                    }, 300);
                } else {
                    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingButtonCreated', function() {
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                try {                                    	
									window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                                } catch (e) {
                                    console.error('[V2 Global] Launch error:', e);
                                }
                            }
                        }, 300);
                    }, { once: true });
                }
            };

            attemptLaunch();
            return;
        }

        const getPageName = window.getPageName || function() {
            const path = window.location.pathname;
            const segments = path.split('/').filter(s => s);
            return segments.length >= 3 ? segments[2] : 'home';
        };
        const currentPage = getPageName();

        // Don't load on home page (has inline component)
        if (currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '') {
            return;
        }

        window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__ = true;

        // ============================================================
        // TIMING FIX: Wait for both Cookie AND Aura to be ready
        // ============================================================
        function waitForDependencies() {
            return new Promise(function(resolve) {
                var maxAttempts = 20; // 20 attempts * 200ms = 4 seconds max wait
                var attempts = 0;
                var checkInterval = 200;

                function getCookie(name) {
                    const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                    return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : null;
                }

                function checkReady() {
                    attempts++;

                    // Check if ContactSupportChatLocale cookie exists
                    var locale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');

                    // Check if $A is available and can retrieve user data
                    var hasAura = false;
                    var userEmail = '';
                    var userId = '';

                    if (typeof $A !== 'undefined') {
                        try {
                            userEmail = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email') || '';
                            userId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id') || '';
                            hasAura = true;
                        } catch (e) {
                            hasAura = false;
                        }
                    }

                    // Resolve if we have locale (cookie ready) and Aura is available
                    // OR if we've exceeded max attempts (fallback to defaults)
                    if ((locale && hasAura) || attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                        resolve({
                            locale: locale || 'en_US',
                            userEmail: userEmail,
                            userId: userId,
                            isAuth: !!(userEmail && userId)
                        });
                    } else {
                        setTimeout(checkReady, checkInterval);
                    }
                }

                checkReady();
            });
        }

        // Wait for dependencies before loading the script
        waitForDependencies().then(function(userData) {
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.id = 'init-embedded-messaging-v2-global';
            script.src = 'https://mcsg.my.site.com/ESWMcAfeeSearchAgentV21774416155829/assets/js/bootstrap.min.js';

            script.onerror = function() {
                window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
            };

            script.onload = function() {
                try {
                    // Use the userData we already fetched
                    //  FIX: agentforceLocale = ContactSupportChatLocale, endUserLang = csp_user_locale
                    // Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
                    const locale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                   getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                   'en_US';
                    const endUserLocale = getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                         getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                         'en_US';
                    const userEmail = userData.userEmail;
                    const userId = userData.userId;
                    const isAuth = userData.isAuth;

                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.language = locale.replace(/-/g, '_');
                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.displayMode = 'floating';
                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.hideChatButtonOnLoad = false;

                    // Set prechat fields BEFORE init()
                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.prepopulatedPrechatFields = {
                        UserEmail: userEmail,
                        siteUserId: userId,
                        isAuthenticated: isAuth ? 'true' : 'false',
                        agentforceLocale: locale,
						endUserLang: endUserLocale
                    };

                    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingButtonCreated', function() {
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                            }
                        }, 500);
                    }, { once: true });

                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.init(
                        '00DDn000004NSPq',
                        'McAfee_Support_Assistant',
                        'https://mcsg.my.site.com/ESWMcAfeeSearchAgentV21774416155829',
                        { scrt2URL: 'https://mcsg.my.salesforce-scrt.com' }
                    );

                    // Set flag to prevent V1 modal overlay from showing
                    window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ = true;
                    //  Set flag when loading for existing conversation (cross-tab scenario)
                    localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
                    sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');

                    // CRITICAL: Remove fake-inline class (should only be on home page)
                    if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                        document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
                    }

                    window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                } catch (error) {
                    window.__V2_BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                }
            };

            document.body.appendChild(script);
        });
    };
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     V2 PAGE LOAD HANDLER - Ensure Maximize on Reload
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Only run for agenticSearch variation
    if (!window.portalSearchVariation || window.portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    //  RELOAD FIX: On page load, check if we're on home page with active chat
    window.addEventListener('load', function() {
        // Get current page
        const getPageName = window.getPageName || function() {
            const urlSegments = location.pathname.split('/').filter(segment => segment.trim());
            return urlSegments.length >= 3 ? urlSegments[2] : 'home';
        };
        const currentPage = getPageName();
        const isHomePage = currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '';

        if (isHomePage) {
            // Check if there's an active chat
            const chatFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
            const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                  sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

            if (chatFrame || conversationId) {

                // Add fake-inline class if not present
                if (!document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                    document.body.classList.add('fake-inline-chat');
                }

                // Wait for iframe to be ready, then maximize
                let checkAttempts = 0;
                const maxAttempts = 20; // 10 seconds

                const ensureMaximized = () => {
                    checkAttempts++;
                    const frame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');

                    if (frame) {
                        const isMinimized = frame.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                          frame.classList.contains('minimized') ||
                                          !frame.classList.contains('isMaximized');

                        //  ALWAYS maximize on reload, don't trust current state
                        // STRATEGY 1: CSS classes
                        frame.classList.remove('isMinimized', 'minimized');
                        frame.classList.add('isMaximized');

                        const container = document.querySelector('.embedded-messaging');
                        if (container) {
                            container.classList.remove('isMinimized', 'minimized');
                            container.classList.add('isMaximized');
                        }

                        // STRATEGY 2: RPC message
                        try {
                            if (frame.src && frame.contentWindow) {
                                const origin = new URL(frame.src).origin;
                                frame.contentWindow.postMessage({
                                    method: 'remoteCall',
                                    name: 'ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE',
                                    params: []
                                }, origin);
                            }
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.warn('[Page Load] RPC error:', e.message);
                        }

                        // STRATEGY 3: API
                        try {
                            if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                            }
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.warn('[Page Load] API error:', e.message);
                        }

                        // Verify after delay
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            const verifyFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
                            if (verifyFrame) {
                                const stillMin = verifyFrame.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                               verifyFrame.classList.contains('minimized');

                                if (stillMin && checkAttempts < maxAttempts) {
                                    setTimeout(ensureMaximized, 500);
                                } 
                            }
                        }, 800);

                    } else if (checkAttempts < maxAttempts) {
                        // Frame not ready yet, keep checking
                        setTimeout(ensureMaximized, 500);
                    } 
                };

                // Start checking after short delay
                setTimeout(ensureMaximized, 500);
            } 
        }
    }, { once: true });

})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     V2 NAVIGATION OBSERVER
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    if (!window.portalSearchVariation || window.portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    let previousUrl = location.href;

    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
        if (location.href !== previousUrl) {
            previousUrl = location.href;

            const currentPage = window.getPageName ? window.getPageName() : 'home';

            //  FIX: Check BOTH frame existence AND localStorage for conversation detection
            // Both V1 and V2 use same Enhanced Chat V2 embedded service
            const chatFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
            const hasActiveChat = chatFrame && !chatFrame.classList.contains('isMinimized');

            // Check localStorage/sessionStorage for conversation ID (works for both V1 and V2)
            const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                  sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

            // ============================================
            //  FIX #2: Ensure builder version on ANY navigation (agenticSearch only)
            // This fixes overlay issue when navigating between pages
            // ============================================
            function getCookie(name) {
                const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
            }

            const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

            // ============================================
            // NAVIGATING TO HOME PAGE
            // ============================================
            if (currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '') {

                // Check frame existence AND conversation ID for reliable detection
                const hasActiveConversation = hasActiveChat || conversationId;

                if (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && hasActiveConversation) {

                    // Add fake-inline class
                    if (!document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                        document.body.classList.add('fake-inline-chat');
                    } 

                    //  COMPREHENSIVE FIX: Multi-strategy maximize with retries
                    let maxRetries = 15; // 7.5 seconds max
                    let retryCount = 0;

                    const attemptMaximize = () => {
                        retryCount++;
                        const checkFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');

                        if (!checkFrame) {
                            // Frame not yet available, retry
                            if (retryCount < maxRetries) {
                                setTimeout(attemptMaximize, 500);
                            } 
                            return;
                        }

                        const isMinimized = checkFrame.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                          checkFrame.classList.contains('minimized') ||
                                          !checkFrame.classList.contains('isMaximized');

                        if (isMinimized) {

                            //  STRATEGY 1: Update CSS classes
                            checkFrame.classList.remove('isMinimized', 'minimized');
                            checkFrame.classList.add('isMaximized');

                            const messagingContainer = document.querySelector('.embedded-messaging');
                            if (messagingContainer) {
                                messagingContainer.classList.remove('isMinimized', 'minimized');
                                messagingContainer.classList.add('isMaximized');
                            }

                            //  STRATEGY 2: Send RPC maximize message
                            try {
                                if (checkFrame.src && checkFrame.contentWindow) {
                                    const targetOrigin = new URL(checkFrame.src).origin;

                                    checkFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
                                        method: 'remoteCall',
                                        name: 'ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE',
                                        params: []
                                    }, targetOrigin);
                                }
                            } catch (rpcErr) {
                                console.warn('[Navigation] RPC error (non-critical):', rpcErr.message);
                            }

                            //  STRATEGY 3: Try launchChat API
                            try {
                                if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                    window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                                }
                            } catch (apiErr) {
                                console.warn('[Navigation] API error (non-critical):', apiErr.message);
                            }

                            //  STRATEGY 4: Click FAB button if visible
                            const fabButton = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton button');
                            if (fabButton) {
                                try {
                                    setTimeout(() => {
                                        fabButton.click();
                                    }, 100);
                                } catch (clickErr) {
                                    console.warn('[Navigation] FAB click error (non-critical):', clickErr.message);
                                }
                            }

                            //  Verify after delay and retry if needed
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                const verifyFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
                                if (verifyFrame) {
                                    const stillMinimized = verifyFrame.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                                          verifyFrame.classList.contains('minimized');

                                    if (stillMinimized && retryCount < maxRetries) {
                                        setTimeout(attemptMaximize, 500);
                                    } 
                                }
                            }, 800);

                        } 
                    };

                    // Start maximize process with initial delay
                    setTimeout(attemptMaximize, 300);

                } else {
                    // Remove class if present (shouldn't be, but safeguard)
                    if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                        document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
                    }
                }
                return;
            }

            // ============================================
            // NAVIGATING AWAY FROM HOME PAGE
            // ============================================

            // Remove fake-inline class when leaving home
            if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
            }

            // CRITICAL: Remove inline styles from chat iframe to restore floating behavior
            // chatFrame already declared above, reuse it
            if (chatFrame) {

                // Remove all our custom properties to let Salesforce's floating styles take over
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('position');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('top');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('left');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('transform');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('bottom');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('right');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('width');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('max-width');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('height');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('max-height');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('border-radius');
                chatFrame.style.removeProperty('box-shadow');

            }

            // If no conversation, don't load bot
            if (!conversationId) {
                return;
            }

            //  NEW: Check if conversation has ended
            const conversationEnded = sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                     localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true';
            if (conversationEnded) {
                return;
            }

            // If not allowed page, don't load bot
            if (!window.isV2BotAllowedOnCurrentPage()) {
                return;
            }

            // Load bot globally for non-home pages with existing conversation
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (typeof window.loadV2BotGlobally === 'function') {
                    window.loadV2BotGlobally();
                }
            }, 500);
        }
    });

    observer.observe(document, { subtree: true, childList: true });
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     IPHONE NAVIGATION FIX - Monitor navigation back to home page
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Only run for agenticSearch variation
    const portalSearchVariation = (function() {
        const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith('apex__portalSearchName='));
        return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
    })();

    if (!portalSearchVariation || portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    // Helper to safely get from storage
    function getFromStorageSafely(key) {
        try {
            return localStorage.getItem(key) || sessionStorage.getItem(key);
        } catch (e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Monitor for navigation back to home page
     * Re-apply fake-inline-chat class if bot is active
     */
    window.monitorHomePageNavigation = function() {
        let lastPathname = window.location.pathname;

        function handleNavigationChange() {
            const pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split('/').filter(s => s);
            const currentPage = pathSegments.length >= 3 ? pathSegments[2] : 'home';
            const isHomePage = currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '';

            // If navigating to home page and bot is active
            if (isHomePage) {
                const hasConversation = getFromStorageSafely('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                const chatActive = getFromStorageSafely('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

                if (hasConversation || chatActive) {
                    // Wait for LWC to mount, then ensure class is present
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        if (!document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                            document.body.classList.add('fake-inline-chat');
                        }
                    }, 500);
                }
            } else {
                // Navigating away from home - ensure class is removed
                if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                    document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
                }
            }
        }

        // Poll for pathname changes (backup detection)
        setInterval(function() {
            if (window.location.pathname !== lastPathname) {
                lastPathname = window.location.pathname;
                handleNavigationChange();
            }
        }, 500);
    };

    // Start monitoring
    window.monitorHomePageNavigation();
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     CSS INJECTION - Prevent overlay for V2 + Fake Inline Styling
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Only inject styles for agenticSearch variation
    if (!window.portalSearchVariation || window.portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // MINIMAL CSS INJECTION
    // All positioning CSS is now in chatbot-unified-styles.css
    // Only inject what MUST be dynamic or cannot be in external CSS
    // ============================================================
    function injectMinimalStyles() {
        if (document.getElementById('agentic-chat-minimal-styles')) {
            return;
        }

        const style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = 'agentic-chat-minimal-styles';
        style.textContent = `
            /* Positioning context for absolutely positioned chat iframe */
            body.fake-inline-chat {
                position: relative !important;
                overflow: visible !important;
            }

            /* Body scroll enabled */
            body:has(c-agentic-embedded-chat-window) {
                overflow: auto !important;
            }

            /* Center alignment - remove contentbody padding on home page */
            body.agenticSearch .contentbody:has(c-agentic-embedded-chat-window) {
                padding-left: 0 !important;
                padding-right: 0 !important;
                max-width: 100% !important;
                width: 100% !important;
                margin-bottom: 1rem !important;
            }

            /* ✅ FIX: Center iframe on home page when navigating back */
            /* This ensures chat window is displayed inline/centered, not in right corner */
            body[data-portal-search="agenticSearch"].fake-inline-chat iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame {
                position: absolute !important;
                top: 16% !important;
                left: 50% !important;
                transform: translateX(-50%) !important;
                max-width: 960px !important;
                width: 100% !important;
                box-sizing: border-box !important;
                overflow: hidden !important;
            }

            /* ✅ NEW: Image alignment fix for inline chat */
            /* Ensures images in chat messages don't overflow or misalign */
            /* NOTE: Do NOT override position, top, left, or transform - those are set above */
            body[data-portal-search="agenticSearch"].fake-inline-chat iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame.isMaximized {
                box-sizing: border-box !important;
                overflow-x: hidden !important;
                overflow-y: auto !important;
                contain: size layout paint !important;
            }

            /* AGGRESSIVE: Scale down if content is too wide */
            /* Combine with existing transform to avoid overriding the centering */
            body[data-portal-search="agenticSearch"].fake-inline-chat iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame[data-overflow="true"] {
                transform: translateX(-50%) scale(0.95) !important;
                transform-origin: top center !important;
            }

            /* Mobile responsive image handling */
            @media (max-width: 768px) {
                body[data-portal-search="agenticSearch"].fake-inline-chat iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame {
                    max-width: 100% !important;
                }
            }

        `;

        document.head.appendChild(style);
    }

    injectMinimalStyles();

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', injectMinimalStyles);
    }

    // All positioning CSS is now handled by chatbot-unified-styles.css
    // No inline style manipulation needed
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     iOS KEYBOARD FIX - Dynamic Viewport Height Calculator
     Fixes Issue #2: iPhone keyboard covering input box
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Detect iOS
    const isIOS = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) ||
                  (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);

    const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 768;

    if (!isIOS || !isMobile) {
        return;
    }

    /**
     * Calculate actual viewport height and set CSS variable
     * This provides fallback for browsers that don't support dvh
     */
    function updateViewportHeight() {
        const vh = window.innerHeight * 0.01;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vh', `${vh}px`);
    }

    // Initial calculation
    updateViewportHeight();

    // Update on resize (keyboard show/hide triggers this)
    let resizeTimer;
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
        resizeTimer = setTimeout(updateViewportHeight, 150);
    });

    // Update on orientation change
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function() {
        // Delay to let orientation change complete
        setTimeout(updateViewportHeight, 300);
    });

    /**
     * Monitor for chat iframe interaction
     * Since iframe is cross-origin, we can't detect focus inside it
     * But we can detect touches on the iframe element itself
     */
    function monitorChatInteraction() {
        const chatIframe = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');

        if (!chatIframe) {
            // Chat not loaded yet, try again
            setTimeout(monitorChatInteraction, 1000);
            return;
        }

        // Detect when user taps the iframe (likely tapping input)
        chatIframe.addEventListener('touchstart', function() {
            setTimeout(updateViewportHeight, 300);
        }, { passive: true });
    }

    // Start monitoring
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', monitorChatInteraction);
    } else {
        monitorChatInteraction();
    }

    // Also listen for Salesforce events
    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingReady', function() {
        setTimeout(monitorChatInteraction, 500);
    });

})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     ENHANCED IMAGE OVERFLOW FIX for Inline Chat
     Addresses: Images wider than chat container causing scrollbars
     Issue: Costco/McAfee activation images overflowing chat window
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Only apply to agenticSearch inline view
    if (!window.portalSearchVariation || window.portalSearchVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
        return;
    }

    console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Initializing enhanced image fix...');

    /**
     * Monitor iframe for size issues and apply fixes
     */
    function monitorAndFixIframe() {
        const iframe = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');

        if (!iframe) {
            return false;
        }

        // Check if we're in inline view
        const isInlineView = document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat');

        if (!isInlineView) {
            return false;
        }

        console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Applying fixes to iframe...');

        // 🔧 CRITICAL FIX: Don't apply aggressive inline styles if position/transform are already set by CSS
        // This preserves proper positioning for authenticated users after logo click
        const hasExistingPosition = iframe.style.position ||
                                   window.getComputedStyle(iframe).position === 'absolute';
        const hasExistingTransform = iframe.style.transform ||
                                    window.getComputedStyle(iframe).transform !== 'none';

        // If CSS positioning is already handling the layout, skip aggressive inline manipulation
        if (hasExistingPosition && hasExistingTransform) {
            console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Skipping inline fixes - CSS positioning is active');
            return false;
        }

        // 1. Force iframe to use border-box sizing and hide horizontal overflow
        iframe.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('overflow-x', 'hidden', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('overflow-y', 'auto', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('max-width', '960px', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('width', '100%', 'important');

        // CRITICAL: Force scrollWidth to respect container
        // This prevents the iframe from expanding beyond its container
        iframe.style.setProperty('contain', 'size layout paint', 'important');

        // 2. Try to inject CSS into iframe content (if same-origin)
        try {
            const iframeDoc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow?.document;

            if (iframeDoc && !iframeDoc.getElementById('custom-image-fix')) {
                const style = iframeDoc.createElement('style');
                style.id = 'custom-image-fix';
                style.textContent = `
                    /* Force all images to respect container width */
                    img {
                        max-width: 100% !important;
                        height: auto !important;
                        width: auto !important;
                        display: block !important;
                        object-fit: contain !important;
                        margin: 8px auto !important;
                    }

                    /* Message content constraints */
                    [class*="message"],
                    [class*="content"],
                    .embeddedMessagingConversationDefaultUI {
                        max-width: 100% !important;
                        overflow-x: hidden !important;
                        box-sizing: border-box !important;
                    }

                    /* Force scrollbar to be on parent, not content */
                    body {
                        overflow-x: hidden !important;
                    }

                    /* Specific fix for message containers */
                    * {
                        max-width: 100%;
                        box-sizing: border-box;
                    }
                `;

                iframeDoc.head.appendChild(style);
                console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] ✅ Successfully injected CSS into iframe');
                return true;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('[Image Overflow Fix] ⚠️ Cannot access iframe content (cross-origin):', e.message);
        }

        // 3. AGGRESSIVE FIX: Always constrain iframe content size
        // This works even when cross-origin restrictions prevent CSS injection
        const maxWidth = Math.min(960, window.innerWidth - 32);

        // Force iframe to have exact dimensions
        iframe.style.setProperty('max-width', maxWidth + 'px', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('width', '100%', 'important');
        iframe.style.setProperty('min-width', '320px', 'important');

        // Check if content is wider than container
        const iframeWidth = iframe.offsetWidth;
        const contentWidth = iframe.scrollWidth;

        console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Iframe width:', iframeWidth, 'Content width:', contentWidth);

        if (contentWidth > iframeWidth) {
            console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] ⚠️ Content exceeds container, applying scale fix');

            // Calculate scale factor to fit content
            const scale = iframeWidth / contentWidth;

            // Apply scale transform + adjust height to compensate
            if (scale < 1 && scale > 0.5) {
                // Reset any existing transform first
                const currentTop = iframe.style.top || '16%';

                iframe.style.setProperty('transform', `translateX(-50%) scale(${scale})`, 'important');
                iframe.style.setProperty('transform-origin', 'top center', 'important');

                // Increase iframe height to compensate for scale
                const currentHeight = iframe.offsetHeight;
                const adjustedHeight = Math.floor(currentHeight / scale);
                iframe.style.setProperty('height', adjustedHeight + 'px', 'important');

                console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] ✅ Applied scale:', scale, 'Adjusted height:', adjustedHeight);
            }
        } else {
            console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] ✅ Content fits within container, no scaling needed');
        }

        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Apply fixes multiple times with delays to catch dynamic content
     */
    function applyFixesWithRetry() {
        // Immediate
        monitorAndFixIframe();

        // After delays (to catch dynamically loaded images)
        setTimeout(monitorAndFixIframe, 500);
        setTimeout(monitorAndFixIframe, 1000);
        setTimeout(monitorAndFixIframe, 2000);
        setTimeout(monitorAndFixIframe, 3000);
    }

    // Listen for Salesforce chat events
    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingReady', function() {
        console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Chat ready, applying fixes...');
        applyFixesWithRetry();
    });

    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingMaximized', function() {
        console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Chat maximized, applying fixes...');
        applyFixesWithRetry();
    });

    window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted', function() {
        console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Conversation started, applying fixes...');
        applyFixesWithRetry();
    });

    // Monitor for iframe creation
    const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
        mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
            mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {
                if (node.nodeType === 1 && node.classList?.contains('embeddedMessagingFrame')) {
                    console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] Iframe detected via mutation observer');
                    applyFixesWithRetry();
                }
            });
        });
    });

    observer.observe(document.body, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true
    });

    // Initial check on load
    if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
        applyFixesWithRetry();
    } else {
        window.addEventListener('load', applyFixesWithRetry);
    }

    // Monitor for window resize (mobile orientation change)
    let resizeTimeout;
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimeout);
        resizeTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
            if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                monitorAndFixIframe();
            }
        }, 250);
    });

    console.log('[Image Overflow Fix] ✅ Enhanced fix initialized');
})();
</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     ANDROID KEYBOARD FIX - Adjust iframe height when keyboard appears
     Fixes: Pixel/Android devices where keyboard covers input box
     ============================================================ -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // Detect Android
    const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 768;

    if (!isAndroid || !isMobile) {
        return;
    }

    // Check if agenticSearch bot is active
    function isAgenticSearchActive() {
        return window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ === true ||
               localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
               sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';
    }

    /**
     * Adjust iframe height when keyboard appears
     * V1: Resize iframe to fit visible viewport
     * V2: Do nothing - let native scrolling handle keyboard
     */
    function adjustForKeyboard() {
        const iframe = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
        if (!iframe) return;

        const isAgenticSearch = isAgenticSearchActive();

        // agenticSearch: Skip all adjustments - let browser handle keyboard naturally
        if (isAgenticSearch) {
            return;
        }

        // globalSearch ONLY: Adjust iframe when keyboard appears
        if (window.visualViewport) {
            const viewportHeight = window.visualViewport.height;
            const windowHeight = window.innerHeight;
            const keyboardHeight = windowHeight - viewportHeight;
            const viewportOffsetTop = window.visualViewport.offsetTop;

            if (keyboardHeight > 100) {
                // Keyboard is visible - make iframe fit visible viewport
                iframe.style.setProperty('height', `${viewportHeight}px`, 'important');
                iframe.style.setProperty('max-height', `${viewportHeight}px`, 'important');
                iframe.style.setProperty('top', `${viewportOffsetTop}px`, 'important');
            } else {
                // Keyboard hidden - restore full height
                iframe.style.setProperty('height', 'auto', 'important');
                iframe.style.setProperty('max-height', 'none', 'important');
                iframe.style.setProperty('top', '0px', 'important');
            }
        }
    }

    // Listen for viewport changes (keyboard show/hide)
    if (window.visualViewport) {
        window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', adjustForKeyboard);
        window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll', adjustForKeyboard);
    }

    // Also listen for window resize as fallback
    let resizeTimer;
    window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
        resizeTimer = setTimeout(adjustForKeyboard, 150);
    });

    // Monitor when chat iframe loads
    function monitorChatIframe() {
        const iframe = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
        if (!iframe) {
            setTimeout(monitorChatIframe, 1000);
            return;
        }
        adjustForKeyboard();

        //  NEW: Detect when user taps iframe (likely tapping input)
        // This helps trigger proper keyboard handling
        iframe.addEventListener('touchstart', function() {
            // Wait for keyboard to appear, then adjust
            setTimeout(adjustForKeyboard, 300);
            setTimeout(adjustForKeyboard, 600);
        }, { passive: true });

        //  NEW: Also try to scroll iframe into view when it's tapped
        iframe.addEventListener('touchstart', function() {
            // Scroll iframe to top of viewport
            setTimeout(function() {
                iframe.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }, 100);
        }, { passive: true, once: true });

        // Listen for Salesforce chat events
        window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingReady', function() {
            setTimeout(adjustForKeyboard, 500);
        });
    }

    // Start monitoring
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', monitorChatIframe);
    } else {
        monitorChatIframe();
    }

})();
</script>

    <!-- Global Utility Functions -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
      // Make getPageName globally accessible for Adobe Launch Script and other scripts
      window.getPageName = function() {
          const urlSegments = location.pathname.split('/').filter(segment => segment.trim());
          return urlSegments.length >= 3 ? urlSegments[2] : 'home';
      };
  </script>

  <!-- STEP 1: Bot Selection Control -->
 <script type="text/javascript">
      (function() {
          'use strict';

          function getCookie(name) {
              const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
              return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
          }

          function setCookie(name, value, days) {
              const expires = days ? `; expires=${new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString()}` : '';
              document.cookie = `${name}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}${expires}; path=/`;
          }

          // CRITICAL: Make decision function reusable and globally accessible
          window.evaluateBotDecision = function evaluateBotDecision() {
              const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
              //  FIX: Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
              const cspUserLocale = (getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                    getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                    'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();
              const contactSupportChatLocale = (getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                               getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                               'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();

              // Check if current page should always use standard bot
              const pageName = window.getPageName();
              const overridePages = ['tech-master-support', 'business'];
              const shouldUseStandardBot = overridePages.includes(pageName);

              // If on override page, set cookie to globalsearch
              if (shouldUseStandardBot) {
                  setCookie('apex__portalSearchName', 'globalsearch', 1);
              }

              // UPDATED: Removed locale restrictions - agenticSearch works for ALL locales
              // Override: Show standard bot on specific pages even if cookie is agenticSearch
              const shouldShowAgentforce = (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' &&
                                           !shouldUseStandardBot);

              window.__SHOW_AGENTFORCE__ = shouldShowAgentforce;
              window.__SHOW_STANDARD_BOT__ = !shouldShowAgentforce;

              return shouldShowAgentforce;
          }

          // Evaluate initially
          const shouldShowAgentforce = window.evaluateBotDecision();

          if (shouldShowAgentforce) {
              // Agentforce mode - DON'T remove elements, just block Einstein bot script
              
              window.__STANDARD_BOT_DISABLED__ = true;

              // Key strategy: Einstein bot is blocked via loadBootstrapMinJs() check
              // Agentforce component will load its own bootstrap naturally
              // NO element removal - both use same CSS classes, can't distinguish safely

          } else {

              window.__AGENTFORCE_DISABLED__ = true;
              window.__STOP_REMOVING_ELEMENTS__ = false;

              // Hide Agentforce ONLY (not standard Einstein bot)
              const style = document.createElement('style');
              style.id = 'agentforce-blocker-style';
              style.innerHTML = `
                  embeddedmessaging-embedded-messaging[data-component-id*="SearchAgent"],
                  c-embedded-messaging[data-component-id*="SearchAgent"],
                  embeddedmessaging-embedded-messaging[data-component-id*="agentforceBot"],
                  c-embedded-messaging[data-component-id*="agentforceBot"] {
                      display: none !important;
                      visibility: hidden !important;
                  }
              `;
              document.head.appendChild(style);

              const removeAgentforceLightningComponentOnly = () => {
                  if (window.__STOP_REMOVING_ELEMENTS__) {
                      return false;
                  }

                  const selectors = [
                      'embeddedmessaging-embedded-messaging',
                      'c-embedded-messaging'
                  ];

                  let removed = 0;
                  selectors.forEach(selector => {
                      document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el => {
                          const componentId = el.getAttribute('data-component-id') || '';
                          // ONLY remove Agentforce (SearchAgent), NOT standard bot (embeddedMessaging)
                          if (componentId.includes('SearchAgent')) {
                              el.remove();
                              removed++;
                          }
                      });
                  });

                  return removed > 0;
              };

              removeAgentforceLightningComponentOnly();

              const removalInterval = setInterval(() => {
                  if (window.__STOP_REMOVING_ELEMENTS__) {
                      clearInterval(removalInterval);
                      return;
                  }
                  removeAgentforceLightningComponentOnly();
              }, 100);

              window.__ALLOW_STANDARD_BOT__ = function() {
                  window.__STOP_REMOVING_ELEMENTS__ = true;
              };

              let bootstrapBlocked = true;
              Object.defineProperty(window, 'embeddedservice_bootstrap', {
                  get: function() {
                      if (bootstrapBlocked) {
                          return {
                              settings: {},
                              init: function() {
                              }
                          };
                      }
                      return window.__real_embeddedservice_bootstrap__;
                  },
                  set: function(value) {
                      if (!bootstrapBlocked) {
                          window.__real_embeddedservice_bootstrap__ = value;
                      } 
                  },
                  configurable: true
              });

              window.__UNBLOCK_BOOTSTRAP__ = function() {
                  window.__ALLOW_STANDARD_BOT__();
                  bootstrapBlocked = false;

                  try {
                      delete window.embeddedservice_bootstrap;
                      delete window.__real_embeddedservice_bootstrap__;
                  } catch (e) {
                      console.error('⚠️ Error unblocking:', e);
                  }
              };
          }

          // ============================================================
          // NEW: Listen for cookie changes and re-evaluate
          // ============================================================
          let lastCookieValue = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

          const checkCookieInterval = setInterval(() => {
              const currentCookieValue = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

              if (currentCookieValue && currentCookieValue !== lastCookieValue) {
                  lastCookieValue = currentCookieValue;

                  // Re-evaluate decision
                  const newDecision = window.evaluateBotDecision();

                  // If decision changed, reload page to apply correct bot
                  if (newDecision !== shouldShowAgentforce) {

                      // Check if we already reloaded
                      const reloadFlag = sessionStorage.getItem('apex__botReload');
                      if (reloadFlag !== 'true') {
                          sessionStorage.setItem('apex__botReload', 'true');
                          window.location.reload();
                      } else {
                          sessionStorage.removeItem('apex__botReload');
                      }
                  }
              }
          }, 200); // Check every 200ms for first 15 seconds

          // Extend cookie polling from 5 seconds to 15 seconds
          // This accounts for slower Aura component initialization
          setTimeout(() => {
              clearInterval(checkCookieInterval);
          }, 15000);

          // Runtime detection and recovery - monitor for wrong bot after cookie is set
          setTimeout(() => {
              const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
              //  FIX: Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
              const cspUserLocale = (getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                    getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                    'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();
              const contactSupportChatLocale = (getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                               getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                               'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();
              const pageName = window.getPageName();
              const overridePages = ['tech-master-support', 'business'];
              const shouldUseStandardBot = overridePages.includes(pageName);

              // If on override page, set cookie to globalsearch
              if (shouldUseStandardBot) {
                  setCookie('apex__portalSearchName', 'globalsearch', 1);
              }
              // UPDATED: Removed locale restrictions - agenticSearch works for ALL locales
              const shouldBeAgentforce = (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' &&
                                         !shouldUseStandardBot);

              // CRITICAL: Check if V2 is already active - don't reload if V2 conversation exists
              const isV2FlagSet = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                 sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                 window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ === true;

              // Also check if CURRENT session should be using Agentforce (not just if flag exists)
              const portalSearchNameCookie = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
              const isCurrentlyAgenticSearch = portalSearchNameCookie === 'agenticSearch';

              if (isV2FlagSet && isCurrentlyAgenticSearch) {
                  return;
              }

              // Check if Einstein bot loaded when it should be Agentforce
              const einsteinLoaded = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton') &&
                                    !window.__SHOW_AGENTFORCE__;

              if (shouldBeAgentforce && einsteinLoaded) {
                  if (!sessionStorage.getItem('bot_reload_attempted')) {
                      sessionStorage.setItem('bot_reload_attempted', 'true');
                      setTimeout(() => {
                          sessionStorage.removeItem('bot_reload_attempted');
                      }, 60000); // Clear flag after 1 minute
                      location.reload();
                  }
              }
          }, 10000); // Check after 10 seconds

      })();
  </script>

  <!--  NEW FIX: Prevent standard bot (Einstein/globalSearch) auto-launch after locale change -->
  <script>
  (function preventStandardBotAutoLaunchAfterLocaleChange() {
      'use strict';

      function getCookie(name) {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
      }

      // Only run if NOT agenticSearch (standard bot)
      const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
      if (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch') {
          return; // V2 logic handles this
      }

      // Check if locale just changed
      const localeJustChanged = sessionStorage.getItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
      const changeTime = sessionStorage.getItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');

      if (localeJustChanged === 'true' && changeTime) {
          const changeAge = Date.now() - parseInt(changeTime);

          if (changeAge < 5000) {
              // Clear flags
              sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_JUST_CHANGED');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('LOCALE_CHANGE_TIME');

              // Clear Einstein bot conversation IDs to prevent auto-launch
              localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
              localStorage.removeItem('ESW_MINIMIZED_TEXT');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('ESW_MINIMIZED_TEXT');
          }
      }
  })();
  </script>
