  <!-- Agentforce Retracting Fix Code START --->
 <script>
      // Track if onEmbeddedMessagingReady has been called before
      window.agentforce_ready_count = window.agentforce_ready_count || 0;

      window.addEventListener("onEmbeddedMessagingReady", () => {
          embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.disableStreamingResponses = true;

          // Get cookie value
          function getCookie(name) {
              const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
              return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
          }

          // Check if this is Agentforce mode
          const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
          const cspUserLocale = (getCookie('csp_user_locale') || 'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();
          const isAgentforceMode = portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch';

          // CRITICAL: DON'T clear conversation ID if it's a V2 conversation
          // Only clear if it's Einstein bot contamination
          if (isAgentforceMode && window.agentforce_ready_count === 0) {

              // Check if there's an existing conversation ID
              const existingConversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                            sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

              if (existingConversationId) {
                  // CRITICAL: Check if this is a V2 conversation before clearing
                  // V2 conversations have special flags set
                  const isV2Chat = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                  sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                  window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ === true ||
                                  document.body.classList.contains('v2-force-loaded');

                  // Also check if we're in a cross-tab scenario
                  const chatState = localStorage.getItem('agentforce_chat_state');
                  let isActiveCrossTab = false;
                  if (chatState) {
                      try {
                          const state = JSON.parse(chatState);
                          const isRecent = (Date.now() - state.timestamp) < 30000;
                          isActiveCrossTab = state.open && isRecent;
                      } catch (e) {
                          console.error('[Agentforce Ready] Error parsing chat state:', e);
                      }
                  }

                  // Only clear if it's NOT a V2 conversation AND not in cross-tab scenario
                  if (!isV2Chat && !isActiveCrossTab) {

                      try {
                          localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                          sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                          localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_DEPLOYMENT_INFO');
                          sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_DEPLOYMENT_INFO');

                          // Clear other Einstein bot keys
                          ['ESW_MINIMIZED_TEXT', 'ESW_UNREAD_MESSAGE_COUNT', 'LA_ESW_UNREAD_MSG_CNT',
                           'CHASITOR_SERIALIZED_KEY', 'SFDC_CHAT_DATA', 'LA_MINIMIZED', 'LA_SESSION_ID'].forEach(key => {
                              localStorage.removeItem(key);
                              sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
                          });

                      } catch (e) {
                          console.error('[Agentforce Ready] ❌ Error clearing session:', e);
                      }
                  } 
              } 
          }

          // Increment ready count
          window.agentforce_ready_count++;

          // If this is the second+ time onEmbeddedMessagingReady fires, it means conversation was ended
          if (isAgentforceMode && window.agentforce_ready_count > 1) {
              // Dispatch a custom event to signal conversation ended
              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('agentforce_conversation_reset'));
          }

          if (isAgentforceMode) {

              // Check if $A is available
              if (typeof $A === 'undefined') {
                  return;
              }

              // Get authenticated user email (same as Einstein bot)
              const userEmail = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email');
              const userId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id');

              // CRITICAL: Get locale from cookie to preserve it
              function getCookie(name) {
                  const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                  return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : null;
              }

              //  CRITICAL FIX: Wait for ContactSupportChatLocale cookie when onEmbeddedMessagingReady fires
              // This happens when user clicks "Start New Conversation" button
              function updatePrechatWithLocale() {
                  //  agentforceLocale = ContactSupportChatLocale, endUserLang = csp_user_locale
                  const agentforceLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');

                  if (!agentforceLocale) {

                      let attempts = 0;
                      const maxAttempts = 20; // 2 seconds max
                      const checkInterval = setInterval(function() {
                          attempts++;
                          const locale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');

                          if (locale) {
                              clearInterval(checkInterval);
                              //  FIX: Get endUserLang with fallback chain when locale is found
                              const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || locale || 'en_US';
                              setPrechatFields(locale, endUserLang);
                          } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                              clearInterval(checkInterval);
                              //  FIX: Use csp_user_locale as fallback for both fields
                              const fallbackLocale = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || 'en_US';
                              setPrechatFields(fallbackLocale, fallbackLocale);
                          }
                      }, 100);
                  } else {
                      //  FIX: Get endUserLang with fallback chain
                      const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || agentforceLocale || 'en_US';
                      setPrechatFields(agentforceLocale, endUserLang);
                  }
              }

              // Helper to set prechat fields
              function setPrechatFields(agentforceLocale, endUserLang) {

                  if (userEmail && userEmail !== '' && userEmail !== 'null' && userId && userId !== '') {
                      try {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields({
                              UserEmail: userEmail,
                              isAuthenticated: 'true',
                              siteUserId: userId,
                              agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
                              endUserLang: endUserLang
                          });
                      } catch (error) {
                          console.error('[Agentforce Ready] ❌ Error setting email:', error);
                      }
                  } else {
                      // Set as anonymous explicitly
                      try {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields({
                              UserEmail: '',
                              isAuthenticated: 'false',
                              siteUserId: '',
                              agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
                              endUserLang: endUserLang
                          });
                      } catch (error) {
                          console.error('[Agentforce Ready] ❌ Error setting anonymous fields:', error);
                      }
                  }
              }

              // Call the update function
              updatePrechatWithLocale();
          }
      });

      //  CRITICAL FIX: Also update prechat fields when conversation actually starts
      // This catches "Start New Conversation" button clicks and reinitiations
      window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted', function(event) {

          if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.prechatAPI) {
              return;
          }

          function getCookie(name) {
              const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
              return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : null;
          }

          // Get current locale from cookies
          const agentforceLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');
          //  FIX: Use fallback chain for endUserLang to prevent blank values
          const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || agentforceLocale || 'en_US';
          const userEmail = $A?.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email') || '';
          const userId = $A?.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id') || '';

          // Use agentforceLocale if available, otherwise fall back to endUserLang
          const finalAgentforceLocale = agentforceLocale || endUserLang;

          try {
              const prechatFields = {
                  agentforceLocale: finalAgentforceLocale,
                  endUserLang: endUserLang
              };

              // Add auth fields if user is authenticated
              if (userEmail && userEmail !== '' && userEmail !== 'null' && userId && userId !== '') {
                  prechatFields.UserEmail = userEmail;
                  prechatFields.isAuthenticated = 'true';
                  prechatFields.siteUserId = userId;
              } else {
                  prechatFields.UserEmail = '';
                  prechatFields.isAuthenticated = 'false';
                  prechatFields.siteUserId = '';
              }

              window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields(prechatFields);
          } catch (error) {
              console.error('[Conv Started] ❌ Error updating prechat fields:', error);
          }
      });

      // Monitor for user sign-in and update chat authentication
      (function monitorAuthenticationChanges() {
          let lastKnownEmail = null;
          let lastKnownUserId = null;
          let checkCount = 0;
          const maxChecks = 120; // Check for 2 minutes (120 * 1000ms = 2 minutes)

          function checkAuthenticationStatus() {
              checkCount++;

              // Stop checking after max attempts
              if (checkCount > maxChecks) {
                  clearInterval(authCheckInterval);
                  return;
              }

              // Check if $A is available
              if (typeof $A === 'undefined') {
                  return;
              }

              // Check if bootstrap is available
              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.prechatAPI) {
                  return;
              }

              // Check if this is Agentforce mode
              function getCookie(name) {
                  const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                  return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
              }

              const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
              const cspUserLocale = (getCookie('csp_user_locale') || 'en_US').replace(/-/g, '_').trim();
              const isAgentforceMode = portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch';

              if (!isAgentforceMode) {
                  return;
              }

              // Get current user email and ID
              const currentEmail = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email');
              const currentUserId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id');

              // Initialize on first check
              if (lastKnownEmail === null) {
                  lastKnownEmail = currentEmail || '';
                  lastKnownUserId = currentUserId || '';
                  return;
              }

              // Check if user just signed in (email changed from empty to having value)
              const wasAnonymous = !lastKnownEmail || lastKnownEmail === '' || lastKnownEmail === 'null';
              const isNowAuthenticated = currentEmail && currentEmail !== '' && currentEmail !== 'null' &&
                                        currentUserId && currentUserId !== '';

              if (wasAnonymous && isNowAuthenticated) {

                  // Update the prechat fields with authenticated user info
                  try {
                      // Get locale from cookie
                      function getCookie(name) {
                          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : null;
                      }
                      //  FIX: agentforceLocale = ContactSupportChatLocale, endUserLang = csp_user_locale
                      // Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
                      const agentforceLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                              getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                              'en_US';
                      const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                         getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                         'en_US';

                      embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields({
                          UserEmail: currentEmail,
                          isAuthenticated: 'true',
                          siteUserId: currentUserId,
                          agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,  //  FIX: Include locale when user signs in
                          endUserLang: endUserLang
                      });
                  } catch (error) {
                      console.error('[Auth Monitor] ❌ Error updating authentication:', error);
                  }

                  // Update last known values
                  lastKnownEmail = currentEmail;
                  lastKnownUserId = currentUserId;

                  // Stop monitoring after successful update (user won't sign out and in again)
                  clearInterval(authCheckInterval);
              }
          }

          // Check every second
          const authCheckInterval = setInterval(checkAuthenticationStatus, 1000);
      })();
  </script>
  <!-- Agentforce Retracting Fix Code END  --->


  <!-- Agentic AI knowledge search START - Simplified chat launch -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
      // Set page name as data attribute on body
      (function() {
          // Use global getPageName function
          const getPageName = window.getPageName;

          const setPageDataAttribute = () => {
              const pageName = getPageName();
              document.body.setAttribute('data-page-name', pageName);
          };

          // Set on load
          if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
              document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setPageDataAttribute);
          } else {
              setPageDataAttribute();
          }

          // Update on URL changes (for SPA navigation)
          let previousUrl = location.href;
          const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
              if (location.href !== previousUrl) {
                  previousUrl = location.href;
                  setPageDataAttribute();
              }
          });

          observer.observe(document, { subtree: true, childList: true });
      })();
  </script>

<script>
      (function() {
          'use strict';
          let pendingSearchQuery = null;
          let conversationStarted = false;
          let messageSendAttempts = 0;
          const MAX_SEND_ATTEMPTS = 5;

          document.addEventListener('launchMIAWChat', function(event) {
              const searchQuery = event.detail?.searchQueryParam;
              if (!searchQuery || searchQuery.trim() === '') {
                  return;
              }

              pendingSearchQuery = searchQuery;
              messageSendAttempts = 0;

              try {
                  sessionStorage.setItem('agentforce_search_query', searchQuery);
                  window.agentforce_search_query = searchQuery;
              } catch (e) {
                  console.error('[Agentforce] Error storing search query:', e);
              }

              const portalVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
              if (portalVariation !== 'agenticSearch') {
                  return;
              }

              // Check if chat window exists and its state
              const chatFrame = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
              const isChatOpen = chatFrame && chatFrame.classList.contains('isMaximized');

              // If conversation is active, send message immediately (works for both open and minimized chat)
              if (conversationStarted && window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.sendTextMessage) {
                  launchChat(searchQuery);
                  // Wait a moment for chat window to open/maximize, then send
                  setTimeout(function() {
                      if (pendingSearchQuery) {
                          sendMessageToAgent(pendingSearchQuery);
                      }
                  }, 800);
              } else if (isChatOpen && !conversationStarted) {
                  // Chat is open but conversation was ended - start new conversation
                  launchChat(searchQuery);
                  // Use retry logic to ensure message gets sent
                  retryMessageSend();
              } else {
                  // Chat is closed or no active conversation - start fresh
                  launchChat(searchQuery);
                  // Use retry logic for new conversation
                  retryMessageSend();
              }
          });

          function sendNotificationToChat(message) {
              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI) {
                  return false;
              }

              try {
                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.sendTextMessage(message);
                  return true;
              } catch (error) {
                  return false;
              }
          }

          // Retry logic to ensure message gets sent after conversation starts
          function retryMessageSend() {
              if (!pendingSearchQuery || messageSendAttempts >= MAX_SEND_ATTEMPTS) {
                  if (messageSendAttempts >= MAX_SEND_ATTEMPTS) {
                      console.error('[Agentforce] Max send attempts reached, giving up');
                      pendingSearchQuery = null;
                  }
                  return;
              }

              messageSendAttempts++;

              setTimeout(function() {
                  // If conversationStarted handler already took over, let it handle the send
                  if (conversationStartedHandlerExecuted && greetingTimeoutId) {
                      return;
                  }

                  // Check if conversation has started
                  if (conversationStarted && pendingSearchQuery) {
                      sendMessageToAgent(pendingSearchQuery);
                  } else if (pendingSearchQuery) {
                      // Try again
                      retryMessageSend();
                  }
              }, 1500); // Wait 1.5 seconds between attempts
          }

          let greetingTimeoutId = null;
          let conversationStartedHandlerExecuted = false;

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted', function(event) {
              conversationStarted = true;
              conversationStartedHandlerExecuted = true;

              //  CROSS-TAB FIX: Manually save conversation ID to localStorage
              try {
                  // ✅ FIXED: Get conversation ID from storage first (most reliable)
                  // AgentForce Messaging doesn't expose conversationId on bootstrap object
                  const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                       sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                       event?.detail?.conversationId;
                  if (conversationId) {
                      localStorage.setItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID', conversationId);
                      sessionStorage.setItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID', conversationId);
                  } 
              } catch (e) {
                  console.error('[Conv Started] Error saving conversation ID:', e);
              }

              //  NEW FIX: Update prechat fields with CURRENT locale when new conversation starts
              // This catches cases where "Start New Conversation" was clicked inside iframe
              function getCookie(name) {
                  const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                  return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
              }

              try {
                  //  FIX: agentforceLocale = ContactSupportChatLocale, endUserLang = csp_user_locale
                  // Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
                  const currentLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                       getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                       'en_US';
                  const currentEndUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                            getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                            'en_US';


                  // Note: This happens AFTER conversation already started, so it's for logging/verification
                  // The actual fix is in markConversationAsEnded() which updates BEFORE next conversation
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[Conv Start] Error checking locale:', error);
              }

              //  Clear ALL "ended" flags when a new conversation starts
              sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
              localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
              localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');

              //  CRITICAL FIX: Only set V2 flags if this is actually a V2 conversation
              // Check builder version for logging purposes
              // Set agenticSearch active flags when conversation starts
              const portalSearch = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
              if (portalSearch === 'agenticSearch') {
                  localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
                  sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
                  window.__V2_FORCE_LOADED__ = true;
              }

              // Handle pending search query
              if (pendingSearchQuery) {

                  // Clear any existing timeout
                  if (greetingTimeoutId) {
                      clearTimeout(greetingTimeoutId);
                      greetingTimeoutId = null;
                  }

                  // Set new timeout - wait for greeting message first
                  greetingTimeoutId = setTimeout(function() {
                      if (pendingSearchQuery) {
                          sendMessageToAgent(pendingSearchQuery);
                      }
                  }, 7000);
              }
          });

          let greetingSent = false;

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingMessage', function(event) {
              if (!greetingSent && pendingSearchQuery && event.detail?.message?.type === 'StaticContent') {
                  if (greetingTimeoutId) {
                      clearTimeout(greetingTimeoutId);
                      greetingTimeoutId = null;
                  }
                  greetingSent = true;
                  setTimeout(function() {
                      if (pendingSearchQuery) {
                          sendMessageToAgent(pendingSearchQuery);
                      }
                  }, 500);
              }
          });

          // ============================================================
          // CONVERSATION END DETECTION - Set flag for page load cleanup
          // ============================================================

          //  NEW FIX: Refresh prechat fields when user interacts after conversation ends
          // This fixes the issue where endUserLang is blank when user types "hi" to restart
          function setupMessageInterceptor() {
              // Do nothing for now - removed postMessage override that was breaking cross-tab
              console.log('[Conv End] Message interceptor disabled to preserve cross-tab functionality');
          }

          function setupPrechatRefreshOnInteraction() {
              function getCookie(name) {
                  const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                  return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
              }

              function refreshPrechatFields() {
                  if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.prechatAPI?.setHiddenPrechatFields) {
                      return;
                  }

                  // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Use stored values from previous conversation (same logic as LWC)
                  const storedLocale = window.__last_conversation_locale__;
                  const storedEndUserLang = window.__last_conversation_endUserLang__;

                  let agentforceLocale, endUserLang;

                  if (storedLocale && storedEndUserLang) {
                      // Use stored values from previous conversation
                      agentforceLocale = storedLocale;
                      endUserLang = storedEndUserLang;
                  } else {
                      // Fallback to cookies (first conversation)
                      agentforceLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                        getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                        'en_US';
                      endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                   getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                   'en_US';
                  }

                  //  CRITICAL FIX: Include ALL prechat fields, not just locale
                  const userEmail = (typeof $A !== 'undefined') ? $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email') : '';
                  const userId = (typeof $A !== 'undefined') ? $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id') : '';
                  const isAuth = userEmail && userId ? 'true' : 'false';

                  try {
                      //  CRITICAL FIX: Update settings.language too (SDK might use this for agentforceLocale)
                      if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.settings) {
                          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.language = agentforceLocale.replace(/-/g, '_');
                      }

                      //  CRITICAL FIX: Update BOTH prepopulatedPrechatFields AND setHiddenPrechatFields
                      // prepopulatedPrechatFields is what SDK uses for new conversations
                      const fields = {
                          UserEmail: userEmail || '',
                          siteUserId: userId || '',
                          isAuthenticated: isAuth,
                          agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
                          endUserLang: endUserLang
                      };

                      // Update settings.prepopulatedPrechatFields (used for new conversations)
                      if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.settings) {
                          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.prepopulatedPrechatFields = fields;
                      }

                      // Also call setHiddenPrechatFields (for running conversation)
                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields(fields);
                  } catch (error) {
                      console.error('[Conv End] Error refreshing prechat fields:', error);
                  }
              }

              // Call refresh immediately
              setTimeout(refreshPrechatFields, 1000);

              // Also refresh periodically for 10 seconds (in case SDK resets them)
              let refreshCount = 0;
              const refreshInterval = setInterval(function() {
                  refreshCount++;
                  refreshPrechatFields();
                  if (refreshCount >= 10) { // Refresh every second for 10 seconds
                      clearInterval(refreshInterval);
                  }
              }, 1000);
          }

          // ✅ OLD markConversationAsEnded function removed
          // Conversation end is now handled in RPC hook (onEmbeddedMessagingReady listener above)
          // which immediately clears CONVERSATION_ID and CHAT_ACTIVE from localStorage


          // Listen for all possible conversation end events
          // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Hook into RPC message handler to detect conversation end
          // V2 uses RPC (not postMessage), so we need to intercept RPC calls
          (function() {

              // Wait for RPC system to be ready
              const checkRPC = setInterval(function() {
                  if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap && window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.rpcAPI) {
                      clearInterval(checkRPC);

                      // Store original handler
                      const originalProcessMessage = window.addEventListener;

                      // Intercept all messages to catch RPC calls
                      window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
                          try {
                              const data = event.data;
                              if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
                                  // Check for cwcsetconversationstatus in RPC messages
                                  if (data.method === 'cwcsetconversationstatus' || data.eventName === 'cwcsetconversationstatus') {
                                      if (data.detail && data.detail.conversationStatus) {
                                          const status = data.detail.conversationStatus;

                                          if (status === 'ended' || status === 'closed' || status === 'inactive') {
                                              markConversationAsEnded('RPC_cwcsetconversationstatus_' + status);
                                          }
                                      }
                                  }
                              }
                          } catch (error) {
                              // Ignore errors
                          }
                      }, true); // Use capture phase to get it early
                  }
              }, 100);

              // Stop checking after 10 seconds
              setTimeout(function() {
                  clearInterval(checkRPC);
              }, 10000);
          })();

          // ✅ DEBUG: Log ALL window events to find conversation end event
          const originalAddEventListener = window.addEventListener;
          window.addEventListener = function(type, listener, options) {
              return originalAddEventListener.call(this, type, listener, options);
          };

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationEnded', function(event) {
              conversationStarted = false;
              conversationStartedHandlerExecuted = false;
              greetingSent = false;
              pendingSearchQuery = null;
              messageSendAttempts = 0;
              if (greetingTimeoutId) {
                  clearTimeout(greetingTimeoutId);
                  greetingTimeoutId = null;
              }

              // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Only clear conversation ID, KEEP AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE
              // This allows bot to auto-launch on reload/cross-tab but start NEW conversation
              localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
          });

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationClosed', function(event) {

              // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Only clear conversation ID, KEEP AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE
              // This allows bot to auto-launch on reload/cross-tab but start NEW conversation
              localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
              sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
          });

          // ============================================================
          // ADDITIONAL CONVERSATION END DETECTION via postMessage/RPC
          // ============================================================
          (function() {

              let endEventHandled = false;

              // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Listen for cwcsetconversationstatus and minimize chat on "ended"
              window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
                  if (!event.data || typeof event.data !== 'object') {
                      return;
                  }

                  const data = event.data;

                  // Detect conversation status = ended via RPC
                  if ((data.method === 'cwcsetconversationstatus' || data.event === 'cwcsetconversationstatus') &&
                      data.data && data.data.detail && data.data.detail.conversationStatus === 'ended') {

                      // Minimize chat window after short delay
                      setTimeout(function() {

                          // Try utilAPI
                          if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.minimizeChat) {
                              try {
                                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.minimizeChat();
                              } catch (error) {
                                  console.log('[Conv End] ⚠️ utilAPI failed:', error.message);
                              }
                          }

                          // Send ESW_APP_MINIMIZE via RPC
                          const chatFrame = document.querySelector('iframe[id*="salesforce"], iframe[name*="messaging"]');
                          if (chatFrame && chatFrame.contentWindow) {
                              try {
                                  chatFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
                                      type: 'rpc-call',
                                      event: 'ESW_APP_MINIMIZE',
                                      id: 'minimize-' + Date.now()
                                  }, '*');
                              } catch (error) {
                                  console.log('[Conv End] ⚠️ RPC failed:', error.message);
                              }
                          }

                          // Update CSS classes
                          if (chatFrame) {
                              chatFrame.classList.remove('isMaximized');
                              chatFrame.classList.add('isMinimized');
                          }
                      }, 2000); // Wait 2 seconds so user sees end message
                  }
              });

              // Listen for postMessage events from Salesforce iframe
              window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
                  // Only process Salesforce messages
                  if (!event.origin || !event.origin.includes('salesforce')) {
                      return;
                  }

                  try {
                      let data = event.data;

                      if (typeof data === 'string') {
                          try {
                              data = JSON.parse(data);
                          } catch (e) {
                              return;
                          }
                      }

                      if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
                          return;
                      }

                      // Detect SSE connection ended
                      if (data.method === 'EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_SSE_CONNECTION_ENDED_EVENT' ||
                          data.eventType === 'EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_SSE_CONNECTION_ENDED_EVENT' ||
                          data.type === 'EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_SSE_CONNECTION_ENDED_EVENT') {

                          if (!endEventHandled) {
                              endEventHandled = true;
                              // Clear only conversation ID, keep CHAT_ACTIVE
                              localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                              sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                              setTimeout(function() {
                                  endEventHandled = false;
                              }, 3000);
                          }
                          return;
                      }

                      // Detect conversation status changes
                      if ((data.method === 'cwcsetconversationstatus' || data.eventName === 'cwcsetconversationstatus') &&
                          data.detail && data.detail.conversationStatus) {

                          const status = data.detail.conversationStatus;

                          if (status === 'ended' || status === 'closed' || status === 'inactive') {
                              if (!endEventHandled) {
                                  endEventHandled = true;

                                  // Clear only conversation ID, keep CHAT_ACTIVE
                                  localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                                  sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

                                  setTimeout(function() {
                                      endEventHandled = false;
                                  }, 3000);
                              }
                              return;
                          }
                      }

                  } catch (error) {
                      // Silently ignore parsing errors
                  }
              });

          })();

          // ============================================================
          // PAGE LOAD: Clear session if conversation was ended
          // ============================================================
          // ✅ NO PAGE LOAD CLEANUP NEEDED
          // Cleanup happens immediately when conversation ends (in RPC hook above)

          // BACKUP: Also reset on our custom event (fires when onEmbeddedMessagingReady is called again)
          window.addEventListener('agentforce_conversation_reset', function(event) {
              conversationStarted = false;
              conversationStartedHandlerExecuted = false;
              greetingSent = false;
              pendingSearchQuery = null;
              messageSendAttempts = 0;
              if (greetingTimeoutId) {
                  clearTimeout(greetingTimeoutId);
                  greetingTimeoutId = null;
              }
          });

          // ============================================================
          // CAPTURE AND RESEND USER MESSAGES AFTER TIMEOUT
          // ============================================================
          (function() {

              let lastUserMessage = null;
              let lastUserMessageTime = 0;
              let conversationRestartDetected = false;
              let userInitiatedRestart = false;

              // Listen for conversation start - detect if user-initiated
              window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted', function(event) {  

                  // Check if conversation was ended recently
                  const chatEnded = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' ||                sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' || localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                   sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true';

                  if (chatEnded) {
                      conversationRestartDetected = true;

                      // Clear ended flags now that conversation is starting
                      localStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
                      sessionStorage.removeItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED');
                      localStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');
                      sessionStorage.removeItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED');

                      // Check if we have a recent user message to resend
                      const timeSinceLastMessage = Date.now() - lastUserMessageTime;
                      if (lastUserMessage && timeSinceLastMessage < 10000) { // Within last 10 seconds
                          userInitiatedRestart = true;

                          // Wait for greeting message, then resend user's message
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              if (lastUserMessage && window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.sendTextMessage) {
                                  try {
                                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.sendTextMessage(lastUserMessage);

                                      // Clear the stored message
                                      lastUserMessage = null;
                                      lastUserMessageTime = 0;
                                  } catch (error) {
                                      console.error('[Message Recovery] ❌ Error resending message:', error);
                                  }
                              }
                              conversationRestartDetected = false;
                              userInitiatedRestart = false;
                          }, 7500); // Wait 7.5 seconds for greeting to load
                      } else {
                          conversationRestartDetected = false;
                      }
                  }
              });

              // Listen for ALL messages to try to capture user messages
              window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingMessage', function(event) {
                  try {
                      if (!event || !event.detail || !event.detail.message) {
                          return;
                      }

                      const message = event.detail.message;
                      const messageType = message.type;
                      const messageText = message.text || message.content || '';

                      // Try to identify user messages (not bot/system messages)
                      // User messages might have type 'Text' or similar
                      if (messageType && messageType !== 'StaticContent' && messageText) {
                          // Check if this might be a user message
                          // (StaticContent is greeting, we want actual user text)
                          const isLikelyUserMessage = messageType === 'Text' ||
                                                     messageType === 'UserMessage' ||
                                                     message.sender === 'EndUser' ||
                                                     message.author === 'EndUser';

                          if (isLikelyUserMessage || (!conversationStarted && messageText.length > 0)) {
                              lastUserMessage = messageText;
                              lastUserMessageTime = Date.now();
                          }
                      }
                  } catch (error) {
                      console.error('[Message Recovery] Error processing message event:', error);
                  }
              });

              // Additional: Monitor for postMessage events from iframe that might contain user input
              window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
                  // Only process Salesforce messages
                  if (!event.origin || !event.origin.includes('salesforce')) {
                      return;
                  }

                  try {
                      let data = event.data;

                      if (typeof data === 'string') {
                          try {
                              data = JSON.parse(data);
                          } catch (e) {
                              return;
                          }
                      }

                      if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
                          return;
                      }

                      // Look for user message attempts in postMessage data
                      if (data.messageText || data.userMessage || data.text) {
                          const messageText = data.messageText || data.userMessage || data.text;
                          if (messageText && typeof messageText === 'string' && messageText.length > 0) {
                              lastUserMessage = messageText;
                              lastUserMessageTime = Date.now();
                          }
                      }
                  } catch (error) {
                      // Silently ignore parsing errors
                  }
              });

          })();

          function launchChat(searchQuery) {
              if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap === 'undefined') {
                  waitForEmbeddedService(function() {
                      launchChat(searchQuery);
                  });
                  return;
              }

              // CRITICAL: Also check if prechatAPI and utilAPI are available
              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI || !window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI) {
                  waitForEmbeddedService(function() {
                      launchChat(searchQuery);
                  });
                  return;
              }

              //  FIX: Wait for ContactSupportChatLocale cookie before setting prechat fields
              // Prevents defaulting to en_US when cookie is temporarily unavailable
              const agentforceLocale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');

              if (!agentforceLocale) {

                  // Wait for cookie with timeout
                  let attempts = 0;
                  const maxAttempts = 20; // 2 seconds max (100ms * 20)
                  const checkInterval = setInterval(function() {
                      attempts++;
                      const locale = getCookie('ContactSupportChatLocale');

                      if (locale) {
                          clearInterval(checkInterval);
                          // Retry launchChat with cookie now available
                          launchChat(searchQuery);
                      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                          clearInterval(checkInterval);
                          // Timeout - use fallback
                          const fallbackLocale = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || 'en_US';
                          // Continue with fallback
                          proceedWithLaunch(fallbackLocale, searchQuery);
                      }
                  }, 100);
                  return; // Exit and wait for cookie
              }

              // Cookie available - proceed immediately
              proceedWithLaunch(agentforceLocale, searchQuery);
          }

          //  NEW: Separated launch logic to avoid code duplication
          function proceedWithLaunch(agentforceLocale, searchQuery) {
			  const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') || agentforceLocale || 'en_US';

              // Check if $A is available
              if (typeof $A === 'undefined') {
                  // Continue anyway with just locale
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields({
                      agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
					  endUserLang: endUserLang,
                      UserEmail: '',
                      isAuthenticated: false,
                      siteUserId: ''
                  });

                  if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                  }
                  return;
              }

              // Get authenticated user email (same as Einstein bot)
              const userEmail = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email');
              const userId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id');

              // Build prechat fields
              const prechatFields = {
                  agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
				  endUserLang: endUserLang
              };

              // Add email if user is authenticated
              if (userEmail && userEmail !== '' && userEmail !== 'null' && userId && userId !== '') {
                  prechatFields.UserEmail = userEmail;
                  prechatFields.isAuthenticated = true;
                  prechatFields.siteUserId = userId;
              } else {
                  prechatFields.UserEmail = '';
                  prechatFields.isAuthenticated = false;
                  prechatFields.siteUserId = '';
              }

              try {
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields(prechatFields);
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[Agentforce Launch] ❌ Error setting prechat fields:', error);
                  // Continue anyway, try to launch chat
              }

              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                  return;
              }

              try {
                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                  // Diagnostic: Check if chat elements exist after launch
                  setTimeout(() => {
                      const chatFrame = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
                      const chatButton = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton');             const isMaximized = chatFrame?.classList.contains('isMaximized');
                  }, 1000);
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[Agentforce Launch] ❌ Error launching chat:', error);
              }
          } // End of proceedWithLaunch function

          function sendMessageToAgent(searchQuery) {
              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.sendTextMessage) {
				return false;
              }
              try {
                  const result = window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.sendTextMessage(searchQuery);
                  pendingSearchQuery = null;
                  messageSendAttempts = 0; // Reset attempts on success
                  return true;
              } catch (error) {
                  return false;
              }
          }

          function waitForEmbeddedService(callback) {
              let attempts = 0;
              const maxAttempts = 30; // Increased to 15 seconds
              const checkInterval = setInterval(function() {
                  attempts++;
                  // Check if bootstrap AND its APIs are available
                  if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI &&
                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI) {
                      clearInterval(checkInterval);
                      callback();
                  } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                      clearInterval(checkInterval);
                  }
              }, 500);
          }

          function getCookie(name) {
              var cookies = document.cookie.split('; ');
              for (var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
                  var parts = cookies[i].split('=');
                  if (parts[0] === name) {
                      return decodeURIComponent(parts[1]);
                  }
              }
              return null;
          }

          // Use global getPageName function (already defined globally)

      })();
  </script>

  <!-- Agentic AI knowledge search END - Simplified chat launch -->

  <script type="text/javascript">
      // Helper function to set cookie
      function setCookie(name, value, days) {
          const expires = days ? `; expires=${new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString()}` : '';
          document.cookie = `${name}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}${expires}; path=/`;
      }

      // Check if current page should always use standard bot
      const shouldUseStandardBotForPage = () => {
          const pageName = getPageName();
          const overridePages = ['tech-master-support', 'business']; // Pages that always use standard bot
          const isOverridePage = overridePages.includes(pageName);

          // If on override page, set cookie to globalsearch
          if (isOverridePage) {
              setCookie('apex__portalSearchName', 'globalsearch', 1);
          }

          return isOverridePage;
      };

      // Standard chatbot configuration - GLOBAL so it can be reused
      window.__BOT_CONFIG__ = {
          globalSearch: {
                bootstrapMinJs: 'https://mcsg.my.site.com/ESWMcAfeeDigitalAssista1718987617577/assets/js/bootstrap.min.js',

              orgId: '00DDn000004NSPq',

          }
      };

      // Guard flag to prevent double-initialization
      window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;

      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
          // IMPORTANT: Don't exit early - always define functions
          // The functions will check internally before executing
          // DOMContentLoaded fired - setting up Einstein bot functions

          // Initialize embedded messaging (works for both globalSearch and agenticSearch)
          const initEmbeddedMessaging = (variationConfig) => {
              // initEmbeddedMessaging called
              try {
                  const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') ?
                      getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';
                  const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                  const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
                      getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';

                  // Get portal search variation

                  // CRITICAL: Check if current page is in allowed pages for bot display
                  const currentPage = window.getPageName();

                  const isHome = currentPage === 'home';
                  const isAllowedPage = isPageInBotList || isHome;

                  // Settings configured
                  // Hide bot on load if: hideChatBot cookie is true, restricted locale (ONLY for globalSearch), OR not on allowed page
                  // UPDATED: Skip locale restriction check for agenticSearch users
                  const isRestrictedLocale = (portalSearchName !== 'agenticSearch') && ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);

                  if (hideChatBot == 'true' || isRestrictedLocale || (!isAllowedPage && !window.__ON_DEMAND_LAUNCH__)) {
                      embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.hideChatButtonOnLoad = true;
                  } else {
                      embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.hideChatButtonOnLoad = false;
                  }

                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.language = cspuserLocale;
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.init(variationConfig.orgId, variationConfig.eswConfigDevName, variationConfig.siteURL, {
                      scrt2URL: variationConfig.scrt2URL
                  });
              } catch (err) {
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.language = 'en_US';
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.init(variationConfig.orgId, variationConfig.eswConfigDevName, variationConfig.siteURL, {
                      scrt2URL: variationConfig.scrt2URL
                  });

          };

          // Load bootstrap script for standard chatbot - MAKE IT GLOBAL
          window.loadBootstrapMinJs = function() {
              // Guard against double-initialization
              if (window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__) {
                  return;
              }

              // Check if bot already exists and is working
              // CRITICAL: Don't skip if agenticSearch conversation is active - we may need to switch modes or auto-launch
              // No builder version check - routing happens in inbound flow
              const hasConversation = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                     sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

              // Get portal search name early for checks
              const earlyPortalCheck = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
              const agenticActive = hasConversation && earlyPortalCheck === 'agenticSearch';

              if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                  typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined' &&
                  document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton')) {

                  if (!agenticActive) {
                      return;
                  }
              }

              window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = true;

              // CRITICAL: Check agenticSearch cross-tab FIRST, before cookie checks
              // Check for actual conversation ID (no builder version check - routing happens in inbound flow)
              const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                    sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

              // Check if conversation has NOT ended
              const conversationEnded = sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                       localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                       sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true' ||
                                       localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ENDED') === 'true';

              //  CROSS-TAB FIX: Check for active conversation BEFORE checking portal search cookie
              // This allows cross-tab to work even if cookie not loaded yet (agenticSearch = V2 by default)
              // Don't check builder version - both V1 and V2 use agenticSearch, flow determines routing
              const agenticCrossTabEarly = conversationId && !conversationEnded;

              if (agenticCrossTabEarly && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
                  const currentPage = getPageName();
                  const isHomePage = currentPage === 'home';

                  if (!isHomePage) {

                      if (typeof window.loadV2BotGlobally === 'function') {
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              window.loadV2BotGlobally();
                          }, 100);
                      }
                      return;
                  } else {
                      window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                      return;
                  }
              }

              // Get portal search name - needed for normal bot initialization
              const portalSearchName = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');

              // Continue with normal bot initialization logic
              const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
                  getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';

              // ✅ UNIFIED: Load bot for both globalSearch AND agenticSearch
              // Both bots now load in headHTML for consistency

              // If cookie is NOT set, WAIT for Aura to set it
              // This prevents bot from loading before the Aura component decides the variation
              // CRITICAL FIX: Check for any locale, not just en_US, to handle navigation from all pages
              if (!portalSearchName && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
                  window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;

                  // SOLUTION: Poll for cookie with timeout
                  let pollAttempts = 0;
                  const maxPollAttempts = 20; // 10 seconds total (500ms * 20)
                  const pollInterval = setInterval(function() {
                      pollAttempts++;
                      const updatedCookie = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');

                      if (updatedCookie) {
                          // Cookie is now set, clear interval and retry initialization
                          clearInterval(pollInterval);
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              window.loadBootstrapMinJs();
                          }, 500);
                      } else if (pollAttempts >= maxPollAttempts) {
                          // Timeout - default to globalSearch
                          clearInterval(pollInterval);
                          // Set a temporary cookie to avoid infinite polling
                          document.cookie = 'apex__portalSearchName=globalSearch; path=/; max-age=86400';
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              window.loadBootstrapMinJs();
                          }, 500);
                      }
                  }, 500);

                  return; // Exit early, will retry after cookie is set
              }

              // ✅ UNIFIED: Select the correct config based on variation
              let selectedConfig;
              if(portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
                  selectedConfig = window.__BOT_CONFIG__.agenticSearch;
              } else {
                  selectedConfig = window.__BOT_CONFIG__.globalSearch;
              }

              // ✅ UNIFIED APPROACH: agenticSearch uses event-based loading ONLY
              // All agenticSearch bot launches happen via 'agenticSearchLaunchRequested' event
              // LWCs (clickToChat, agenticEmbeddedChatWindow) dispatch events, headHTML handles loading
              if(portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
                  const currentPage = getPageName();
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] agenticSearch detected, currentPage:', currentPage);
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Skipping automatic bot load - unified handler will load on demand');
                  window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                  return; // Exit early - unified handler will load bot when event is dispatched
              }

      // CRITICAL: Stop removing elements and unblock bootstrap
      if (window.__ALLOW_STANDARD_BOT__) {
          window.__ALLOW_STANDARD_BOT__();
      }

      if (window.__UNBLOCK_BOOTSTRAP__) {
          window.__UNBLOCK_BOOTSTRAP__();
      }

              
	      const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') ? getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';
              const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
              const akamaiLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
              const callLocalefromAkamai = getCookieValues('ContactSupportCallLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
              
              if(window.cookiesCheckCount > 10 || (Boolean(chatLocale) && Boolean(cspuserLocale) && Boolean(hideChatBot))) {
                      // Double-check cookie one more time before loading bootstrap
                      const finalPortalCheck = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');

                      // ✅ UNIFIED: Select config based on variation
                      const config = (finalPortalCheck === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage())
                          ? window.__BOT_CONFIG__.agenticSearch
                          : window.__BOT_CONFIG__.globalSearch;

                      console.log('[HeadHTML] Loading bootstrap script for:', finalPortalCheck, 'URL:', config.bootstrapMinJs);

                      // ✅ UNIFIED: Load bootstrap script for BOTH globalSearch AND agenticSearch
                      const script = document.createElement('script');
                      script.src = config.bootstrapMinJs;
                      script.onload = function() {
                          console.log('[HeadHTML] Bootstrap script loaded successfully for:', finalPortalCheck);

                          // ✅ NEW: Set flag if loading for non-home page (like Contact Support)
                          const currentPage = window.getPageName ? window.getPageName() : 'home';
                          if (currentPage !== 'home' && currentPage !== '' && finalPortalCheck === 'agenticSearch') {
                              sessionStorage.setItem('BOT_LAUNCHED_FROM_CONTACT_SUPPORT', 'true');
                              localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
                              sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
                              // ✅ CRITICAL: Store variation with conversation to detect cross-variation conflicts
                              localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_VARIATION', 'agenticSearch');
                              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Set bot active flags and variation for non-home page:', currentPage);
                          } else if (finalPortalCheck === 'globalSearch' || finalPortalCheck === 'globalsearch') {
                              // ✅ CRITICAL: Store variation for globalSearch (Einstein Bot)
                              // This allows detection of variation changes when user switches to agenticSearch
                              localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_VARIATION', 'globalSearch');
                              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Stored variation: globalSearch');
                          }

                          initEmbeddedMessaging(config);
                          // Reset flag after successful init
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                          }, 2000);
                      };
                      script.onerror = function() {
                          console.error('[HeadHTML] Bootstrap script failed to load for:', finalPortalCheck);
                          window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false;
                      };
                      document.body.appendChild(script);
                      console.log('[HeadHTML] Bootstrap script tag appended to DOM for:', finalPortalCheck);

                      if(window.addEventListener) {
                          window.addEventListener("message", handleMessage);
		      }
                      else {
                          window.attachEvent("onmessage", handleMessage);
                      }
                  
              } else {                  
                  window.cookiesCheckCount += 1;
                  window.__BOT_INITIALIZING__ = false; // Reset flag so retry can proceed
                  setTimeout(loadBootstrapMinJs, 1000);
              }
          }

          // SOLUTION: Global callback for Aura component to notify when cookie is set
          // This allows Aura component to immediately trigger bot initialization after setting cookie
          window.notifyBotCookieReady = function(cookieValue) {


              // Small delay to ensure cookie is actually set in browser
              setTimeout(function() {
                  const verifiedCookie = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');


                  if (verifiedCookie) {

                      window.loadBootstrapMinJs();
                  } 
              }, 300);
          };

          // CRITICAL: Callback used by Aura helper to notify when cookie is ready
          // This is called by SupportSiteCustomThemeLayoutHelper.js after setting cookie
          window.__AURA_COOKIE_READY_CALLBACK__ = function() {

              // Small delay to ensure cookie is actually set in browser
              setTimeout(function() {
                  const verifiedCookie = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');

                  if (verifiedCookie) {
                      window.loadBootstrapMinJs();
                  } 
              }, 300);
          };

            // Start loading - but check if we should skip for home page
	setTimeout(function() {
      // Check if this is home page
      const currentPath = window.location.pathname;
      const isHomePage = currentPath === '/' || currentPath === '' ||
                        currentPath === '/s/' || currentPath.endsWith('/s');

      // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Skip ALL auto-initialization on home page
      // Reason: Aura component transitions between variations during page load
      // (globalSearch → agenticSearch → language normalization), causing cookie
      // to be deleted and recreated multiple times. If bot initializes during
      // these transitions, it connects to wrong deployment.
      // Solution: Only initialize via explicit button click after transitions complete.
      if (isHomePage) {
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 🏠 Home page detected - skipping auto-init, bot will load on button click');
          return;
      }

      // Otherwise, proceed with normal bot initialization for non-home pages
      loadBootstrapMinJs();
  }, 1000);
      });

      // ============================================================
      // ✅ UNIFIED BOT LAUNCH HANDLER
      // Central logic for launching agenticSearch bot from ANY component
      // ============================================================

      // Function to load agenticSearch bot bootstrap script
      function loadAgenticSearchBot(page, displayMode) {
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 🚀 Loading agenticSearch bot bootstrap');

          // Get config
          const config = window.__BOT_CONFIG__.agenticSearch;
          const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') || 'en_US';

          // Load bootstrap script
          const script = document.createElement('script');
          script.src = config.bootstrapMinJs;

          script.onload = function() {
              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Bootstrap loaded for agenticSearch');

              // ✅ DEBUG: Check if bootstrap object exists
              setTimeout(function() {
                  if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap === 'undefined') {
                      console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ CRITICAL: embeddedservice_bootstrap not defined after script load');
                      console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Bootstrap script URL:', config.bootstrapMinJs);
                      console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ This means the deployment may not exist or script failed to initialize');

                      // Dispatch error event to LWC to hide spinner
                      document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('agenticSearchLoadFailed', {
                          detail: { error: 'Bootstrap object not created' }
                      }));
                  } else {
                      console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ embeddedservice_bootstrap exists:', typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap);
                      console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ utilAPI exists:', typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI);
                  }
              }, 1000);

              // Set flags
              localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
              sessionStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE', 'true');
              // ✅ CRITICAL: Store variation with conversation to detect cross-variation conflicts
              localStorage.setItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_VARIATION', 'agenticSearch');
              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Stored variation: agenticSearch');

              // ✅ FIX: Create launchExecuted flag as object so it can be passed by reference
              let launchExecuted = { value: false };

              // Initialize bot (pass launchExecuted so event listeners can modify it)
              // Event listeners are now registered INSIDE initAgenticSearchBot before init() is called
              initAgenticSearchBot(page, displayMode, config, 0, launchExecuted);

              // Fallback: If event doesn't fire within 3 seconds, poll for API readiness
              setTimeout(function() {
                  if (launchExecuted.value) return;

                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ⏰ Timeout reached, checking if API is ready');

                  // Poll for utilAPI to be ready (more robust than single attempt)
                  let pollAttempts = 0;
                  const maxPollAttempts = 40; // 20 seconds max (increased from 10)

                  const pollForAPI = setInterval(function() {
                      pollAttempts++;

                      // ✅ VERBOSE: Log what we're checking
                      const bootstrapExists = typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined';
                      const utilAPIExists = bootstrapExists && window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI;
                      const launchChatExists = utilAPIExists && typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat === 'function';

                      console.log('[HeadHTML] 🔍 Poll attempt', pollAttempts, '- bootstrap:', bootstrapExists, 'utilAPI:', !!utilAPIExists, 'launchChat:', launchChatExists);

                      if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI &&
                          typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat === 'function') {

                          // API is ready!
                          clearInterval(pollForAPI);

                          if (!launchExecuted.value && window.__LAUNCH_AFTER_INIT__) {
                              launchExecuted.value = true;

                              try {
                                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ API ready, launching chat via fallback');
                                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ launchChat() executed successfully');
                              } catch (error) {
                                  console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Error in fallback launch:', error);
                              }
                              window.__LAUNCH_AFTER_INIT__ = false;
                          }
                      } else if (pollAttempts >= maxPollAttempts) {
                          clearInterval(pollForAPI);
                          console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ API never became ready after', maxPollAttempts * 500, 'ms');
                          launchExecuted.value = true; // Prevent further attempts

                          // ✅ RECOVERY: Dispatch failure event to LWC to restore search button
                          document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('agenticSearchLoadFailed', {
                              detail: {
                                  error: 'utilAPI timeout',
                                  message: 'Chat service failed to initialize. Please try again.'
                              }
                          }));
                          console.log('[HeadHTML] 📤 Dispatched load failure event to LWC');
                      } else {
                          console.log('[HeadHTML] ⏳ Polling for API... attempt', pollAttempts);
                      }
                  }, 500);
              }, 5000);
          };

          script.onerror = function(error) {
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Failed to load bootstrap script');
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Script URL:', config.bootstrapMinJs);
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Error:', error);

              // Dispatch failure event to LWC to restore search button
              document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('agenticSearchLoadFailed', {
                  detail: {
                      error: 'Script load failed',
                      message: 'Unable to load chat service. Please check your connection and try again.',
                      url: config.bootstrapMinJs
                  }
              }));
          };

          document.body.appendChild(script);
      }

      // Function to initialize agenticSearch bot
      function initAgenticSearchBot(page, requestedDisplayMode, config, retryCount, launchExecuted) {
          retryCount = retryCount || 0;
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 🎬 Initializing agenticSearch bot (attempt ' + (retryCount + 1) + ')');

          // CRITICAL: Check if bootstrap object exists
          if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap === 'undefined') {
              if (retryCount < 10) { // Max 10 retries = 5 seconds
                  console.warn('[HeadHTML] ⚠️ Bootstrap object not found - retrying in 500ms');
                  setTimeout(function() {
                      initAgenticSearchBot(page, requestedDisplayMode, config, retryCount + 1, launchExecuted);
                  }, 500);
              } else {
                  console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Bootstrap object never loaded after 10 retries');
              }
              return;
          }

          // Get locale
          const locale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') || 'en_US';

          // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Register event listeners BEFORE init (not after)
          // The SDK fires events during/after init, so listeners must be ready

          // Listen for onEmbeddedMessagingReady (but don't launch yet - wait for button created)
          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingReady', function() {
              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ onEmbeddedMessagingReady fired - SDK initialized');
              // Don't launch here - API not ready until onEmbeddedMessagingButtonCreated fires
          }, { once: true });

          // Also listen for button created event
          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingButtonCreated', function() {
              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ onEmbeddedMessagingButtonCreated fired');

              if (window.__LAUNCH_AFTER_INIT__ && !launchExecuted.value) {
                  launchExecuted.value = true;

                  setTimeout(function() {
                      try {
                          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                          console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ launchChat() executed after button created');
                      } catch (error) {
                          console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Error in launch:', error);
                      }
