                      window.__LAUNCH_AFTER_INIT__ = false;
                  }, 300);
              }
          }, { once: true });

          // Configure SDK
          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.language = locale.replace(/-/g, '_');

          // CRITICAL: Always use floating mode, CSS handles inline appearance
          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.displayMode = 'floating';
          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.hideChatButtonOnLoad = false;

          // Set prechat fields
          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings.prepopulatedPrechatFields = {
              agentforceLocale: locale,
              endUserLang: getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') || locale
          };

          // Add error listener to catch SDK failures
          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingInitError', function(error) {
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ SDK Init Error:', error);
          });

          // ✅ DIAGNOSTIC: Check what the SDK bootstrap object contains before init
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 🔍 Pre-init diagnostic:', {
              bootstrapExists: typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap,
              settingsExists: typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings,
              hasInitFunction: typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.init === 'function',
              currentSettings: window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings
          });

          // Initialize with try-catch
          try {
              window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.init(
                  '00DDn000004NSPq',
                  'McAfee_Support_Assistant',
                  'https://mcsg.my.site.com/ESWMcAfeeSearchAgentV21774416155829',
                  {
                      scrt2URL: 'https://mcsg.my.salesforce-scrt.com'
                  }
              );
              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Bot initialized (init() called successfully)');

              // ✅ DIAGNOSTIC: Check SDK state immediately after init
              setTimeout(function() {
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] 🔍 Post-init diagnostic (500ms later):', {
                      utilAPIExists: typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI,
                      utilAPIValue: window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI,
                      hasMinimizeMethod: window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI && typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.minimizeChat === 'function',
                      hasLaunchMethod: window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI && typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat === 'function'
                  });
              }, 500);
          } catch (error) {
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Exception during init():', error);
              console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Stack trace:', error.stack);
          }
      }

      window.addEventListener('agenticSearchLaunchRequested', function(event) {
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 🎯 Received launch request:', event.detail);

          const { source, page, displayMode, conversationId } = event.detail || {};

          // Add fake-inline-chat class for home page
          if (page === 'home' || displayMode === 'inline') {
              if (!document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                  document.body.classList.add('fake-inline-chat');
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Added fake-inline-chat class for inline display');
              }
              document.body.setAttribute('data-portal-search', 'agenticSearch');
          } else {
              // Non-home page - remove fake-inline-chat class
              if (document.body.classList.contains('fake-inline-chat')) {
                  document.body.classList.remove('fake-inline-chat');
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Removed fake-inline-chat class for floating display');
              }
          }

          // Check if bot already initialized and ready
          // Use existing AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE flag to determine if bot was loaded before
          const botAlreadyActive = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                   sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

          if (botAlreadyActive &&
              typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
              window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI) {

              console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Bot already initialized, launching chat');

              // Bot exists, just launch it
              try {
                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                  console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ launchChat() called successfully');
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[HeadHTML] ❌ Error launching chat:', error);
              }

              return;
          }

          // Bot not initialized yet - load it
          console.log('[HeadHTML] 📦 Bot not ready, loading bootstrap');

          // Set flag that we want to launch after init
          window.__LAUNCH_AFTER_INIT__ = true;
          window.__LAUNCH_REQUEST_PAGE__ = page;

          // Check cookie
          const portalSearchCookie = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
          if (portalSearchCookie === 'agenticSearch') {
              loadAgenticSearchBot(page, displayMode);
          } else {
              console.warn('[HeadHTML] ⚠️ agenticSearch cookie not set, cannot load bot');
          }
      });

      console.log('[HeadHTML] ✅ Unified launch handler registered');

      // Global variables
      window.botIncludedPages = ['topic','topiccatalog','article','global-search','case','countries-and-phone'];
      window.cookiesCheckCount = 0;
      window.v_hideChatBot = "true";

      // Utility functions
      const checkPreChatFieldsValues = value => value !== undefined && value !== null;

      // Utility function needed for the event listener above
      function getPortalSearchNameCookieValue(name) {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
      }

      // Get Cookie Values (returns 'en_US' default for locale cookies)
      // Make it global so other script tags can use it
      window.getCookieValues = function(name) {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : 'en_US';
      };
      const getCookieValues = window.getCookieValues; // Local alias

      // Get Bot Cookie Values (returns empty string for boolean cookies like hideChatBot)
      const getBotCookieValue = name => {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
      };

      // Use global getPageName function
      const getPageName = window.getPageName;

      // Set preChat Hidden fields
      function callPrechatAPI(event) {
          // Skip if this is agenticSearch variation
          const portalSearchName = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
          const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
              getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';
          //  FIX: Support ALL locales for agenticSearch V2, not just en_US
          if(portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
              return;
          }
		  
        const akamaiLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
        const callLocalefromAkamai = getCookieValues('ContactSupportCallLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
	const logs = (event.detail?.userInfo?.email??'No User Info') +'*'+ 
		$A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email')+'*'+(event.detail?.queueApiName ?? 'NoQueue');
		
		const userDetails = {
	  siteUserId: $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id'),
	  siteUserEmail: event.detail?.userInfo?.email ?? $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email'),
          AkamaiData: Boolean(getCookieValues('akamaiCountryData')) ? decodeURI(getCookieValues('akamaiCountryData')) : null,
	  botLocale: cspuserLocale,
	  siteLocale: Boolean(akamaiLocale) ? akamaiLocale : 'en_US',
	  callLocale: Boolean(callLocalefromAkamai) ? callLocalefromAkamai : 'en_US',
	  siteUserLanguage: getCookieValues('fallback-language'),
	  queueApiName: event.detail?.queueApiName ?? null,
	  caseId: event.detail?.userInfo?.caseid ?? null,
	  customerAccountId: event.detail?.userInfo?.customeraccountid ?? null,
          setPreviousSessionDetails: event.detail?.userInfo?.caseid ? "true" : "false",
          PageUrl: event.detail?.pageUrl ?? null,
          businessHoursLocale: event.detail?.selectedBizHrsLocale ?? null,
          portalLogs : logs.substring(0, 255)
        };
        Object.entries(userDetails).forEach(([key, value]) => {
            if (checkPreChatFieldsValues(value)) {
                embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields({ [key]: value });
            }
        });
    };
    // Launch the chat
    const launchChat = async () => {
	          // Skip if this is agenticSearch variation
          const portalSearchName = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
          const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
              getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';

          //  FIX: Support ALL locales for agenticSearch V2, not just en_US
          if(portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
              // Launch agentic embedded component
              //  FIX: Use fallback chain instead of immediate en_US default
              const agentforceLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                      getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                      'en_US';
			  const endUserLang = getCookie('csp_user_locale') ||
                                 getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') ||
                                 'en_US';

              // Check if we need to allow chat to open (only if chat already exists and user clicked)
              // Don't call this during initial load - only when user explicitly clicks "Start Chat"
              const chatFrame = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
              const chatExists = chatFrame !== null;

              if (window.agentforce_should_minimize && chatExists) {
                  // Call the allowChatToOpen function to take ownership
                  if (typeof window.allowChatToOpen === 'function') {
                      window.allowChatToOpen();
                  }
              } 

              // Check if bootstrap exists
              if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap === 'undefined') {
                  return;
              }

              // Check for authenticated user and build prechat fields
              const prechatFields = {
                  agentforceLocale: agentforceLocale,
				  endUserLang: endUserLang
              };

              // Check if $A is available and get user email
              if (typeof $A !== 'undefined') {
                  const userEmail = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Email');
                  const userId = $A.get('$SObjectType.CurrentUser.Id');

                  // Add email if user is authenticated
                  if (userEmail && userEmail !== '' && userEmail !== 'null' && userId && userId !== '') {
                      prechatFields.UserEmail = userEmail;
                      prechatFields.isAuthenticated = true;
                      prechatFields.siteUserId = userId;
                  } else {
                      prechatFields.UserEmail = '';
                      prechatFields.isAuthenticated = false;
                      prechatFields.siteUserId = '';
                  }
              } else {
                  prechatFields.UserEmail = '';
                  prechatFields.isAuthenticated = false;
                  prechatFields.siteUserId = '';
              }

              try {
                  embeddedservice_bootstrap.prechatAPI.setHiddenPrechatFields(prechatFields);
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[launchChat] ❌ Error setting prechat fields:', error);
              }

              // Check if utilAPI.launchChat is available
              if (!window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                  // SOLUTION: Wait for bot to be ready
                  let attempts = 0;
                  const maxAttempts = 20;
                  const waitInterval = setInterval(function() {
                      attempts++;
                      if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                          clearInterval(waitInterval);
                          window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                          clearInterval(waitInterval);
                      }
                  }, 500);
                  return;
              }

              // Launch chat
              try {
                  window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[launchChat] ❌ Error launching chat:', error);
              }
              return;
          }
          // CRITICAL: Check if bot is initialized, if not, initialize it on-demand
          if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap === 'undefined') {
              
              // Initialize bot first
              if (typeof window.loadBootstrapMinJs === 'function') {
                  // Override the page check temporarily for on-demand launch
                  window.__ON_DEMAND_LAUNCH__ = true;

                  window.loadBootstrapMinJs();

                  // Wait for bot to initialize, then launch
                  let attempts = 0;
                  const maxAttempts = 40; // 20 seconds max (500ms * 40)
                  const waitInterval = setInterval(function() {
                      attempts++;
                      if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat === 'function') {
                          clearInterval(waitInterval);
                          window.__ON_DEMAND_LAUNCH__ = false;
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                      } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                          clearInterval(waitInterval);
                          window.__ON_DEMAND_LAUNCH__ = false;                          
                      }
                  }, 500);
              }
              return;
          }

          // CRITICAL: Validate utilAPI is ready (globalSearch path only)
          // This prevents crash when bot is partially loaded but utilAPI not yet available
          if (!embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI ||
              typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat !== 'function') {
              return;
          }

          const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale') ?
              getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';
          const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();



              try {
                  await embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
              } catch (error) {
                  console.error('[launchChat] ❌ Error launching chat:', error);
              } finally {
              }

      };

      // Event listeners for chatbot - CRITICAL: launchChat must ALWAYS be registered
      const registerBotEventListeners = () => {
          const portalSearchName = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
          const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
              getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';

          // Registering bot event listeners

          // CRITICAL: ALWAYS register launchChat listener
          // The launchChat function handles BOTH Einstein and Agentforce bots
          // Registering launchChat event listener
          window.addEventListener("launchChat", launchChat);

          // STEP 1: If cookie NOT set, skip Einstein-specific listeners
          // Wait for Aura to decide the variation
          if (!portalSearchName && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
              return;
          }

          // STEP 2: If cookie is agenticSearch, skip Einstein-specific listeners
          //  FIX: Support ALL locales for agenticSearch V2, not just en_US
          if (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage()) {
              // Skipping Einstein-specific listeners - AgenticSearch mode
              return;
          }

          // STEP 3: Register Einstein-specific listeners for globalSearch mode
          // Registering Einstein-specific event listeners
          window.addEventListener("onEmbeddedMessagingReady", function(event) {

              callPrechatAPI(event);

              // CRITICAL: Immediately check if bot should be visible on current page
              setTimeout(function() {

                  // CRITICAL: Skip visibility logic if agenticSearch is active
                  //  FIX: Check variation FIRST to prevent globalSearch users from being affected by V2 flags
                  function getCookie(name) {
                      const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                      return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
                  }

                  const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

                  // Check if agenticSearch AND has active V2 session
                  const isV2Active = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                    sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

                  if (portalSearchVariation === 'agenticSearch' && isV2Active) {
                      return; // Let agentic component handle visibility
                  }

                  const currentPage = window.getPageName();
                  const isPageInBotList = window.botIncludedPages ? window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => currentPage === keyword) : false;
                  const isHome = currentPage === 'home';
                  const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                  const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();

                  const shouldHide = hideChatBot == 'true' || (!isPageInBotList && !isHome) || ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);

                  if (shouldHide) {
                      if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton === 'function') {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton();
                      } 
                  } else {
                      if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.showChatButton === 'function') {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.showChatButton();
                      }
                  }
              }, 500); // Small delay to ensure utilAPI is ready

              // ADDITIONAL CHECK: Run again after 2 seconds to catch late bot appearances
              setTimeout(function() {
                  console.log('[Bot Visibility] 🔄 2-second recheck starting...');

                  // CRITICAL: Skip visibility logic if agenticSearch is active
                  //  FIX: Check variation FIRST to prevent globalSearch users from being affected by V2 flags
                  function getCookie(name) {
                      const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                      return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
                  }

                  const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

                  // Check if agenticSearch AND has active V2 session
                  const isV2Active = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                    sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

                  if (portalSearchVariation === 'agenticSearch' && isV2Active) {
                      return; // Let agentic component handle visibility
                  }

                  const currentPage = window.getPageName();
                  const isPageInBotList = window.botIncludedPages ? window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => currentPage === keyword) : false;
                  const isHome = currentPage === 'home';
                  const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                  const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();

                  const shouldHide = hideChatBot == 'true' || (!isPageInBotList && !isHome) || ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);

                  const buttonExists = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton');

                  if (shouldHide && buttonExists) {
                      if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined' &&
                          typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton === 'function') {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton();
                      }
                  } 
              }, 2000); // Check again after 2 seconds
          });
          window.addEventListener("onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted", (event) => {
          });
      };

      // Register listeners (launchChat will ALWAYS be registered)
      registerBotEventListeners();
  </script>

  <!-- Japan/Non-Japan Icon Logic - START -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
      function handleMessage(event) {
          var baseURL = window.location.origin;
          const currentOrigin = 'https://mcsg.my.site.com';
          if(event.origin == currentOrigin && event.data?.botLaunched === 'true') {
              if(getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') == "ja") {
                  document.getElementById('embeddedMessagingFrame')?.contentWindow?.postMessage('JapanLocale', '*');
              }
          }
          if (event.data.name === 'hide-chatbot') {
              window.v_hideChatBot = event.data.payload;
              // Polling mechanism to check for embeddedservice_bootstrap
              let attempts = 0;
              const maxAttempts = 40; // 2 seconds max
              var intervalId = setInterval(function() {
                  attempts++;
                  const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                  if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' && typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined') {
                      clearInterval(intervalId);

                      const portalSearchName = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

                      //  FIX: Skip page restrictions entirely for agenticSearch
                      // agenticSearch manages its own visibility via LWC components
                      if (portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch') {
                          return; // Don't call show/hide for agenticSearch
                      }

                      // For globalSearch only, apply page restrictions
                      if (window.v_hideChatBot == 'false' && (window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => getPageName() === keyword) || getPageName() == 'home')) {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.showChatButton();
                      } else if (window.v_hideChatBot == 'true' || (!window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => getPageName() === keyword) && getPageName() !== 'home')) {
                          embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton();
                      }
                  } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                      clearInterval(intervalId);
                 }
            }, 50); // Check every 100 milliseconds
        }
    }

      // Japan vs Non-Japan icon styling (works for both V1 and V2)
      if(getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') == "ja") {
          var style = document.createElement('style');
          var cssRule =
              // V1 selector (with .embedded-messaging parent)
              '.embedded-messaging:not(:has(.isMaximized)) .embeddedMessagingConversationButton '
              + '{background-image: url(/sfsites/c/resource/McAfeeChatbotResources_V3/images/JapanBotIcon.svg) !important;}'
              + '.embedded-messaging:not(:has(.isMaximized)) .embeddedMessagingIconChat path { display: none;}'
              // V2 selector (direct targeting, works with force-loaded V2)
              + ' .embeddedMessagingConversationButton:not(.isMaximized) '
              + '{background-image: url(/sfsites/c/resource/McAfeeChatbotResources_V3/images/JapanBotIcon.svg) !important;}'
              + ' .embeddedMessagingIconChat path { display: none;}';
          style.appendChild(document.createTextNode(cssRule));
          document.head.appendChild(style);
      } else {
          var style = document.createElement('style');
          var cssRule =
              // V1 selector (with .embedded-messaging parent)
              '.embedded-messaging:not(:has(.isMaximized)) .embeddedMessagingConversationButton '
              + '{background-image: url(/sfsites/c/resource/McAfeeChatbotResources_V3/images/FionaDarkred.svg) !important; background-position: center !important;}'
              + '.embedded-messaging:not(:has(.isMaximized)) .embeddedMessagingIconChat path { display: none;}'
              // V2 selector (direct targeting, works with force-loaded V2)
              + ' .embeddedMessagingConversationButton:not(.isMaximized) '
              + '{background-image: url(/sfsites/c/resource/McAfeeChatbotResources_V3/images/FionaDarkred.svg) !important; background-position: center !important;}'
              + ' .embeddedMessagingIconChat path { display: none;}';
          style.appendChild(document.createTextNode(cssRule));
          document.head.appendChild(style);
      }
  </script>
  <!-- Japan/Non-Japan Icon Logic - END -->

  <!-- URL Change Observer - START -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
      // CRITICAL: Initialize to current URL to prevent observer from running on initial page load (cross-tab)
      let previousUrl = location.href;
      let botVisibilityIntervalId = null; // Track interval to prevent stacking

      // Use global getPageName function
      const getPageName1 = window.getPageName;

	  // Utility function needed for the event listener above
      function getPortalSearchNameCookieValue(name) {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
      }

      // Helper function to set cookie
      function setCookie1(name, value, days) {
          const expires = days ? `; expires=${new Date(Date.now() + days * 864e5).toUTCString()}` : '';
          document.cookie = `${name}=${encodeURIComponent(value)}${expires}; path=/`;
      }

      // Check if current page should always use standard bot
      const shouldUseStandardBotForPage1 = () => {
          const pageName = getPageName1();
          const overridePages = ['tech-master-support', 'business']; // Pages that always use standard bot
          const isOverridePage = overridePages.includes(pageName);

          // If on override page, set cookie to globalsearch
          if (isOverridePage) {              
              setCookie1('apex__portalSearchName', 'globalsearch', 1);
          }

          return isOverridePage;
      };

      const observer = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
          if (location.href !== previousUrl) {
              previousUrl = location.href;
              const currentPage = getPageName1();
              // Re-evaluate bot decision for new page (this updates window.__SHOW_AGENTFORCE__ based on current page)
              if (typeof window.evaluateBotDecision === 'function') {
                  window.evaluateBotDecision();
              }

              // Check portal search name - EARLY CHECK to protect agenticSearch users
              const portalSearchName = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
              const cspuserLocale = getCookieValues('csp_user_locale') ?
                  getCookieValues('csp_user_locale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim() : 'en_US';


              // ============================================================
              // agenticSearch Same-Tab Navigation Handler
              // ============================================================
              if(portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch' && !shouldUseStandardBotForPage1()) {
                  console.log('[URL Observer] agenticSearch detected on page:', currentPage);

                  // Get current page name
                  const isHomePage = currentPage === 'home' || currentPage === '';

                  // ✅ FIX: Load bot on non-home pages EVEN IF no active conversation
                  // This ensures bot is ready when user clicks "Start Chat"
                  if (!isHomePage) {
                      console.log('[URL Observer] Non-home page - ensuring bot is loaded');

                      // ✅ CRITICAL: Check if bot loaded AND utilAPI exists
                      // Bootstrap object may exist from home page but utilAPI could be broken
                      const hasUtilAPI = typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' &&
                                        typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined';

                      if (!hasUtilAPI) {
                          console.log('[URL Observer] Bot not ready (bootstrap:', typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap, 'utilAPI:', typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI, ') - reloading');

                          // ✅ Clear broken bootstrap if it exists
                          if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined') {
                              console.log('[URL Observer] Removing broken bootstrap object');
                              delete window.embeddedservice_bootstrap;
                          }

                          if (typeof window.loadBootstrapMinJs === 'function') {
                              setTimeout(() => window.loadBootstrapMinJs(), 500);
                          }
                      } else {
                          console.log('[URL Observer] Bot already loaded and utilAPI ready');
                      }
                  }

                  // Check: agenticSearch chat must be active (conversation exists)
                  const chatActive = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') ||
                                    sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');

                  // If no active conversation, we're done (bot is loaded but not launched)
                  if (chatActive !== 'true') {
                      console.log('[URL Observer] No active conversation, bot loaded but not launched');
                      return;
                  }

                  // Check if conversation exists
                  const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID') ||
                                        sessionStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');

                  // If navigating AWAY from home page with active conversation
                  if (!isHomePage && conversationId) {

                      // Check if bootstrap exists
                      if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined') {
                          const currentMode = window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings?.displayMode;

                          // If in inline mode, force page reload to switch to embedded mode
                          // This is the only way to switch modes due to Salesforce limitation
                          if (currentMode === 'inline') {
                              // Reload the page - this will load bot fresh in embedded mode
                              window.location.reload();
                              return;
                          } else if (currentMode === 'embedded') {
                              // Check if chat is already expanded
                              const isChatExpanded = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton[aria-expanded="true"]');

                              if (!isChatExpanded) {
                                  // Chat not expanded, launch it

                                  // CRITICAL: Clear minimize flag for V2 only (don't affect V1 behavior)
                                  const isV2Active = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                                    sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

                                  if (isV2Active && window.agentforce_should_minimize) {
                                      window.agentforce_should_minimize = false;
                                  }

                                  // If bootstrap exists with utilAPI, launch directly
                                  // Don't check for button existence - bootstrap handles that internally
                                  if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                      setTimeout(function() {
                                          try {
                                              window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                                          } catch (error) {
                                              console.error('[Nav Observer] Error launching chat:', error);
                                              console.error('[Nav Observer] Error details:', error.message);
                                          }
                                      }, 500);
                                  } 
                              }
                              return; // Exit - bot already in correct mode
                          }
                      }

                      // Bootstrap not loaded yet - load it now
                      // This happens on fresh cross-tab loads or after page reload
                      if (typeof window.loadV2BotGlobally === 'function') {
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              window.loadV2BotGlobally();
                          }, 300);
                      }
                  } else if (isHomePage) {

                      // Clear the mode switch flags
                      sessionStorage.removeItem('v2_mode_switch');
                      sessionStorage.removeItem('v2_mode_switch_reload');

                      // If bot is in embedded mode with active conversation, hide it
                      // The inline component will handle showing the search bar
                      if (typeof window.embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined') {
                          const currentMode = window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.settings?.displayMode;

                          if (currentMode === 'embedded' && conversationId) {

                              // Hide the embedded bot button
                              setTimeout(function() {
                                  if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.hideChatButton) {
                                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton();
                                  }
                              }, 100);

                              // Set a flag so the inline component knows to reload the bot in inline mode when clicked
                              sessionStorage.setItem('v2_switch_to_inline', 'true');
                          }
                      }
                  } 
                  return; // Exit after handling V2 logic
              }

			const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
			const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();

			// CRITICAL: Skip visibility logic if agentic chat is active
			const chatActive = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') ||
			                  sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
			if (chatActive === 'true' && portalSearchName === 'agenticSearch') {
				return; // Exit - V2 already handled above
			}

			// Standard chatbot visibility handling (globalSearch only)
                  // Clear any existing interval before creating new one
                  if (botVisibilityIntervalId) {
                      clearInterval(botVisibilityIntervalId);
                      botVisibilityIntervalId = null;
                  }

                  let attempts = 0;
                  const maxAttempts = 200; // 10 seconds max (200 * 50ms) - increased for bot initialization

                  botVisibilityIntervalId = setInterval(function() {
                      attempts++;
                      const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                      const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();

                      // Check if bot is initialized with utilAPI
                      if (typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap !== 'undefined' && typeof embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI !== 'undefined') {
                          clearInterval(botVisibilityIntervalId);
                          botVisibilityIntervalId = null;

                          // CRITICAL: Skip visibility logic if agentic chat is active
                          function getCookie(name) {
                              const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                              return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
                          }
                          const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');
                          const isV2Active = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                            sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

                          if (portalSearchVariation === 'agenticSearch' && isV2Active) {
                              return;
                          }

                          const currentPageName = getPageName1();
                          const isInBotList = window.botIncludedPages ? window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => currentPageName === keyword) : false;
                          const isHomePage = currentPageName === 'home';
                          const shouldHide = hideChatBot == 'true' || (!isInBotList && !isHomePage) || ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);
                          if (shouldHide) {
                              embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.hideChatButton();
                          }
                          else {
                              embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.showChatButton();
                          }
                      }
                      // Check if bot button exists in DOM (alternative to utilAPI)
                      // V2 Agentforce: cwcmessaging-cwc-fab | V1 Einstein: .embeddedMessagingConversationButton
                      else if (document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton') || document.querySelector('cwcmessaging-cwc-fab')) {
                          clearInterval(botVisibilityIntervalId);
                          botVisibilityIntervalId = null;

                          // Get cookie and check bot type
                          function getCookie(name) {
                              const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
                              return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
                          }
                          const portalSearchVariation = getCookie('apex__portalSearchName');

                          // For agenticSearch (Agentforce), ALWAYS show button - no page restrictions
                          if (portalSearchVariation === 'agenticSearch') {
                              const fabButton = document.querySelector('cwcmessaging-cwc-fab');
                              if (fabButton) {
                                  fabButton.style.display = '';
                                  fabButton.style.visibility = 'visible';
                              }
                              return;
                          }

                          // For globalSearch (Einstein bot), apply page restrictions
                          const botButton = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingConversationButton');
                          if (!botButton) return;

                          const currentPageName = getPageName1();
                          const isInBotList = window.botIncludedPages ? window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => currentPageName === keyword) : false;
                          const isHomePage = currentPageName === 'home';
                          const shouldHide = hideChatBot == 'true' || (!isInBotList && !isHomePage) || ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);

                          if (shouldHide) {
                              botButton.style.display = 'none';
                          }
                          else {
                              botButton.style.display = '';
                          }
                      }
                      else if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                          clearInterval(botVisibilityIntervalId);
                          botVisibilityIntervalId = null;

                          // Check if we should show the bot on this page
                          const currentPage = getPageName1();
                          const isPageInBotList = window.botIncludedPages.some(keyword => currentPage === keyword);
                          const isHome = currentPage === 'home';
                          const chatLocale = getCookieValues('ContactSupportChatLocale')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                          const hideChatBot = getBotCookieValue('hideChatBot')?.replace(/-/g, '_')?.trim();
                          const isRestrictedLocale = ['tr', 'fi', 'no'].includes(chatLocale);

                          const shouldShowBot = (hideChatBot !== 'true') && (isPageInBotList || isHome) && !isRestrictedLocale;

                          if (shouldShowBot) {
                              // CRITICAL: Check which bot version to load based on configuration
                              // Don't load both - only load the correct one based on variation

                              // Check if V2 is active
                              const chatActive = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') ||
                                                sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
                              const isV2Active = chatActive === 'true';

                              // Check portal search variation
                              const portalSearch = getPortalSearchNameCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName');
                              const isAgenticSearch = portalSearch === 'agenticSearch';

                              // Load the correct bot version
                              if (isV2Active && isAgenticSearch) {
                                  // For V2 (agenticSearch)
                                  if (typeof window.loadV2BotGlobally === 'function') {
                                      setTimeout(function() {
                                          window.loadV2BotGlobally();
                                      }, 500);
                                  } 
                              } else {
                                  // For V1 (globalSearch or no V2 flag)
                                  if (typeof window.loadBootstrapMinJs === 'function') {
                                      setTimeout(function() {
                                          window.loadBootstrapMinJs();
                                      }, 500);
                                  } 
                              }
                          } 
                      }
				}, 50);
        }
    });
    const config = { subtree: true, childList: true };
    observer.observe(document, config);
</script>
  <!-- URL Change Observer - END -->

  <!-- Custom FAB Button Manager - START -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
  (function() {
      'use strict';

      function getCookieValue(name) {
          const cookie = document.cookie.split('; ').find(c => c.startsWith(name + '='));
          return cookie ? decodeURIComponent(cookie.split('=')[1]) : '';
      }

      function setupCustomFabButton() {
          const portalSearchName = getCookieValue('apex__portalSearchName') ||
                                   document.body.getAttribute('data-portal-search');

          if (portalSearchName !== 'agenticSearch') {
              return;
          }

          // Track button state to avoid unnecessary DOM operations
          let customButtonExists = false;
          let unreadMessageCount = 0;
          let lastMessageId = null; // Track last message to avoid duplicate counts
          let lastIncrementTime = 0; // Track last time we incremented to throttle rapid events

          // ✅ SALESFORCE FIX: Restore unread count from storage on page load (iOS Safari fix)
          // iOS Safari may reload tabs aggressively, so persist count in localStorage
          try {
              const storedCount = localStorage.getItem('CUSTOM_FAB_UNREAD_COUNT');
              if (storedCount && !isNaN(storedCount)) {
                  unreadMessageCount = parseInt(storedCount);
              }
          } catch (e) {
              // Storage not accessible
          }

          // ✅ RPC EVENT DETECTION: Listen for Salesforce RPC messages (dispatchEventToHost)
          // Salesforce Enhanced Messaging uses RPC communication between iframe and host
          window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
              try {
                  let data = event.data;

                  // Skip if not an object
                  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
                      return;
                  }

                  // Parse if string
                  if (typeof data === 'string') {
                      try {
                          data = JSON.parse(data);
                      } catch (e) {
                          return;
                      }
                  }

                  // Log ALL RPC calls to understand the message flow
                  if (data && data.type === 'rpc-call') {
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] RPC Call detected:', data.event, 'Full data:', data);
                  }

                  // Check if this is an RPC call message from the chat iframe
                  // Format: {type: 'rpc-call', event: 'dispatchEventToHost', data: {eventName: '...', eventData: {...}}}
                  if (data && data.type === 'rpc-call' && data.event === 'dispatchEventToHost' && data.data) {
                      const eventName = data.data.eventName;
                      const eventData = data.data.eventData;

                      // Log all dispatchEventToHost events for debugging
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] dispatchEventToHost - eventName:', eventName, 'Is minimized:', document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame')?.classList.contains('isMinimized'));

                      // Check for any message event (user OR bot)
                      if (eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessageSent' ||
                          eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingMessage' ||
                          eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessageReceived' ||
                          eventName === 'messageReceived') {

                          // Get unique message ID for deduplication
                          const conversationEntry = eventData?.conversationEntry || {};
                          const entryType = conversationEntry.entryType;
                          const sender = conversationEntry.sender; // Object with {role: 'EndUser' or 'Chatbot', ...}
                          const senderRole = sender?.role; // 'EndUser' or 'Chatbot'

                          // ✅ CRITICAL FIX: Only count bot messages, not user messages
                          // sender.role = 'Chatbot' for bot, 'EndUser' for user
                          if (senderRole !== 'Chatbot') {
                              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ⏭️ Skipping - message from user (sender role:', senderRole, ')');
                              return;
                          }

                          // Parse entryPayload to get targetMessageIdentifier (for streaming) or message ID
                          let uniqueMessageId = null;
                          try {
                              const entryPayload = conversationEntry.entryPayload;
                              if (entryPayload) {
                                  const parsed = JSON.parse(entryPayload);

                                  // For StreamingToken: use targetMessageIdentifier (groups all chunks of one response)
                                  if (parsed.streamingToken && parsed.streamingToken.targetMessageIdentifier) {
                                      uniqueMessageId = parsed.streamingToken.targetMessageIdentifier;
                                  }
                                  // For Message: use the message ID
                                  else if (parsed.id) {
                                      uniqueMessageId = parsed.id;
                                  }
                              }
                          } catch (e) {
                              // Fallback to identifier if parsing fails
                              uniqueMessageId = conversationEntry.identifier;
                          }

                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Bot message detected. Type:', entryType, 'Unique ID:', uniqueMessageId, 'Last ID:', lastMessageId, 'Sender role:', senderRole);

                          // Skip if this is a duplicate (same targetMessageIdentifier/messageId)
                          if (uniqueMessageId && uniqueMessageId === lastMessageId) {
                              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ⏭️ Skipping - same message/turn already counted');
                              return;
                          }

                          // Update last message ID
                          lastMessageId = uniqueMessageId;

                          // ✅ FIX: Delay minimized check to allow iframe classes to update
                          // The RPC event fires before the iframe classes are updated
                          setTimeout(function() {
                              const iframe = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
                              const isMinimized = iframe?.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                                 iframe?.classList.contains('minimized');

                              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Delayed check - Is minimized:', isMinimized, 'Current count:', unreadMessageCount);

                              // Increment unread count if chat is minimized (for ANY message - bot or user)
                              if (isMinimized) {
                                  // ✅ THROTTLE: Only increment once per 2 seconds
                                  // Bot responses can trigger multiple message events, so throttle to count as one
                                  const now = Date.now();
                                  const timeSinceLastIncrement = now - lastIncrementTime;

                                  if (timeSinceLastIncrement < 2000) {
                                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ⏭️ Skipping increment - throttled (', timeSinceLastIncrement, 'ms since last)');
                                      return;
                                  }

                                  lastIncrementTime = now;
                                  unreadMessageCount++;
                                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Incrementing badge count to:', unreadMessageCount);

                                  // ✅ iOS Safari fix: Persist to localStorage
                                  try {
                                      localStorage.setItem('CUSTOM_FAB_UNREAD_COUNT', unreadMessageCount.toString());
                                  } catch (e) {
                                      // Storage not accessible
                                  }

                                  updateNotificationBadge();

                                  // ✅ Show "New message" popup notification
                                  showMessagePopup();
                              } else {
                                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Message received but chat is NOT minimized - not incrementing');
                              }
                          }, 100); // 100ms delay to let classes update
                      }

                      // Check for maximize event
                      if (eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingMaximized' ||
                          eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingConversationOpened' ||
                          eventName === 'ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE') {
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Maximize/Open event detected via dispatchEventToHost - clearing badge');
                          clearUnreadCount();
                      }

                      // ✅ Track conversation started (for active conversation indicator)
                      if (eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingConversationStarted') {
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] 🟢 Conversation started event detected');
                          updateActiveConversationIndicator(true);
                      }

                      // ✅ Track conversation ended/closed
                      if (eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingConversationEnded' ||
                          eventName === 'onEmbeddedMessagingConversationClosed' ||
                          eventName === 'cwcresetclienttoinitialstate') {
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] 🔴 Conversation ended/closed event detected:', eventName);
                          updateActiveConversationIndicator(false);
                      }
                  }

                  // Also check for direct ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE RPC calls (not wrapped in dispatchEventToHost)
                  if (data && data.type === 'rpc-call' && data.event === 'ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE') {
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Direct ESW_APP_MAXIMIZE RPC call detected - clearing badge');
                      clearUnreadCount();
                  }

                  // Check for conversation reset/ended events
                  if (data && data.type === 'rpc-call' && data.event === 'cwcresetclienttoinitialstate') {
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] 🔴 Direct conversation reset detected - disabling indicator');
                      updateActiveConversationIndicator(false);
                  }
              } catch (error) {
                  // Silently ignore parsing errors
              }
          });

          // ✅ FALLBACK: Listen for traditional browser events (if they exist)
          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessageReceived', function(event) {
              try {
                  const iframe = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
                  const isMinimized = iframe?.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                     iframe?.classList.contains('minimized');

                  if (isMinimized) {
                      unreadMessageCount++;

                      try {
                          localStorage.setItem('CUSTOM_FAB_UNREAD_COUNT', unreadMessageCount.toString());
                      } catch (e) {
                          // Storage not accessible
                      }

                      updateNotificationBadge();
                  }
              } catch (error) {
                  // Silently ignore
              }
          });

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingConversationOpened', function(event) {
              clearUnreadCount();
          });

          window.addEventListener('onEmbeddedMessagingMaximized', function(event) {
              clearUnreadCount();
          });

          // ✅ NEW: Update notification badge on custom FAB
          function updateNotificationBadge() {
              const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
              if (!customButton) return;

              let badge = customButton.querySelector('.message-notification-badge');

              if (unreadMessageCount > 0) {
                  // Add class to indicate unread messages (stops pulse animation)
                  customButton.classList.add('has-unread-messages');

                  if (!badge) {
                      // Create badge
                      badge = document.createElement('span');
                      badge.className = 'message-notification-badge';
                      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', unreadMessageCount + ' unread messages');
                      customButton.appendChild(badge);
                  }

                  // Update badge count (show max 9+)
                  badge.textContent = unreadMessageCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadMessageCount.toString();
                  badge.style.display = 'block';
              } else if (badge) {
                  // Remove badge if no unread messages
                  badge.remove();
                  customButton.classList.remove('has-unread-messages');
              }
          }

          // ✅ NEW: Update active conversation indicator (pulse animation)
          function updateActiveConversationIndicator(hasActiveConversation) {
              const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
              if (!customButton) {
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ⚠️ Cannot update indicator - button not found');
                  return;
              }

              if (hasActiveConversation) {
                  customButton.classList.add('has-active-conversation');
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Active conversation indicator enabled');
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Button classes:', customButton.className);

                  // Log computed styles to verify CSS is applied
                  setTimeout(() => {
                      const styles = window.getComputedStyle(customButton);
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Computed border:', styles.border);
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Computed animation:', styles.animation);
                  }, 100);
              } else {
                  customButton.classList.remove('has-active-conversation');
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Active conversation indicator disabled');
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Button classes:', customButton.className);
              }
          }

          // ✅ NEW: Get translated "New message" text based on user locale
          function getNewMessageText() {
              // Try to get locale from cookies (same as chat uses)
              let locale = getCookieValue('csp_user_locale') ||
                          'en_US';

              // Normalize locale format (en_US, en-US, en-us, etc.)
              locale = locale.replace('-', '_').toLowerCase();

              // Translations for "New message"
              const translations = {
                  'en_us': 'New message',
                  'es': 'Nuevo mensaje',
                  'pt_br': 'Nova mensagem',
                  'fr': 'Nouveau message',
                  'de': 'Neue Nachricht',
                  'it': 'Nuovo messaggio',
                  'nl': 'Nieuw bericht',
                  'pl': 'Nowa wiadomość',
                  'ja': '新しいメッセージ',
                  'zh_cn': '新消息',
                  'zh_tw': '新訊息',
                  'ko': '새 메시지',
                  'tr': 'Yeni mesaj',
                  'sv': 'Nytt meddelande',
                  'da': 'Ny besked',
                  'no': 'Ny melding',
                  'fi': 'Uusi viesti'
              };

              return translations[locale] || translations['en_us'];
          }

          // ✅ NEW: Show "New message" popup notification
          function showMessagePopup() {
              const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
              if (!customButton) {
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ❌ Custom button not found, cannot show popup');
                  return;
              }

              // Don't show popup if one already exists
              const existingPopup = customButton.querySelector('.message-popup-notification');
              if (existingPopup) {
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Popup already visible, not creating new one');
                  return;
              }

              // Get translated text
              const messageText = getNewMessageText();

              // Create new popup container
              const popup = document.createElement('div');
              popup.className = 'message-popup-notification';
              popup.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
              popup.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');

              // Create text span
              const textSpan = document.createElement('span');
              textSpan.textContent = messageText;
              textSpan.style.flexGrow = '1';
              popup.appendChild(textSpan);

              // Create close button (X icon)
              const closeButton = document.createElement('button');
              closeButton.className = 'message-popup-close';
              closeButton.innerHTML = '&times;'; // × symbol
              closeButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Close notification');
              closeButton.style.background = 'none';
              closeButton.style.border = 'none';
              closeButton.style.color = 'inherit';
              closeButton.style.fontSize = '20px';
              closeButton.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
              closeButton.style.cursor = 'pointer';
              closeButton.style.padding = '0 0 0 12px';
              closeButton.style.marginLeft = '8px';
              closeButton.style.lineHeight = '1';
              closeButton.style.opacity = '0.7';

              // Close button hover effect
              closeButton.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
                  closeButton.style.opacity = '1';
              });
              closeButton.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
                  closeButton.style.opacity = '0.7';
              });

              // Close button click handler
              closeButton.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                  e.stopPropagation(); // Prevent triggering parent click events
                  popup.remove();
                  console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Popup closed by user');
              });

              popup.appendChild(closeButton);

              popup.style.display = 'flex';
              popup.style.alignItems = 'center';
              popup.style.justifyContent = 'space-between';
              customButton.appendChild(popup);

              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Popup created and shown with text:', messageText);
          }

          // ✅ NEW: Clear unread count when chat is maximized
          function clearUnreadCount() {
              unreadMessageCount = 0;
              lastMessageId = null; // Reset message tracking
              lastIncrementTime = 0; // Reset throttle timer

              // ✅ iOS Safari fix: Clear from localStorage
              try {
                  localStorage.removeItem('CUSTOM_FAB_UNREAD_COUNT');
              } catch (e) {
                  // Storage not accessible
              }

              updateNotificationBadge();

              // ✅ Remove popup when chat is maximized
              const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
              if (customButton) {
                  const popup = customButton.querySelector('.message-popup-notification');
                  if (popup) {
                      popup.remove();
                      console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Popup removed on maximize');
                  }
              }

              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ✅ Cleared badge count and reset message tracking');
          }

          setInterval(function() {
              const iframe = document.querySelector('.embeddedMessagingFrame');
              const isMinimized = iframe?.classList.contains('isMinimized') ||
                                 iframe?.classList.contains('minimized');
              const hasActiveChat = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true' ||
                                   sessionStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE') === 'true';

              // ✅ OPTIMIZED: Only check if we have an active chat
              if (!hasActiveChat) {
                  // Remove button if it exists and chat is not active
                  if (customButtonExists) {
                      const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
                      if (customButton) {
                          customButton.remove();
                          customButtonExists = false;
                          unreadMessageCount = 0; // ✅ Reset count
                          updateActiveConversationIndicator(false); // Disable indicator
                      }
                  }
                  return;
              }

              // Chat is active - check if minimized
              if (isMinimized) {
                  // Create button if it doesn't exist
                  if (!customButtonExists) {
                      let customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');

                      if (!customButton) {
                          customButton = document.createElement('button');
                          customButton.id = 'custom-chat-fab';
                          customButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open McAfee Support Chat');
                          customButton.setAttribute('role', 'button');
                          customButton.setAttribute('type', 'button');

                          // ✅ OPTIMIZED: Single click handler with error handling
                          customButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
                              try {
                                  // Clear unread count when user opens chat
                                  clearUnreadCount();

                                  if (window.embeddedservice_bootstrap?.utilAPI?.launchChat) {
                                      window.embeddedservice_bootstrap.utilAPI.launchChat();
                                  }
                              } catch (error) {
                                  console.error('[Custom FAB] ❌ Error launching chat:', error);
                              }
                          });

                          document.body.appendChild(customButton);
                          customButtonExists = true;

                          // ✅ Update badge if there are unread messages
                          updateNotificationBadge();

                          // ✅ Check if there's an active conversation and enable indicator
                          // Check both AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE and EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID
                          // Both must be present for an active conversation
                          const chatActive = localStorage.getItem('AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE');
                          const conversationId = localStorage.getItem('EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID');
                          const hasActiveConversation = chatActive === 'true' && conversationId;

                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] Checking active conversation on button creation:');
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB]   AGENTFORCE_CHAT_ACTIVE:', chatActive);
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB]   EMBEDDED_MESSAGING_CONVERSATION_ID:', conversationId);
                          console.log('[CUSTOM FAB]   hasActiveConversation:', hasActiveConversation);

                          if (hasActiveConversation) {
                              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] 🟢 Active conversation detected - enabling indicator');
                              updateActiveConversationIndicator(true);
                          } else {
                              console.log('[CUSTOM FAB] ⚪ No active conversation - indicator stays at rest');
                          }
                      } else {
                          customButtonExists = true;
                      }
                  }
              } else {
                  // Chat is maximized - clear unread count and remove custom button
                  if (customButtonExists) {
                      clearUnreadCount();

                      const customButton = document.getElementById('custom-chat-fab');
                      if (customButton) {
                          customButton.remove();
                          customButtonExists = false;
                      }
                  }
              }
          }, 300); // Poll every 300ms
      }

      // Initialize immediately if DOM ready
      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setupCustomFabButton);
      } else {
          setupCustomFabButton();
      }
  })();
  </script>
  <!-- Custom FAB Button Manager - END -->

  <!-- Mobile Keyboard Scroll Fix - START -->
  <script type="text/javascript">
  (function() {
      'use strict';

      // Only run on mobile devices
      if (!/iPhone|iPad|iPod|Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent)) {
          return;
      }

      console.log('[Keyboard Fix] Initializing mobile keyboard scroll fix');

      let scrollPosition = 0;
      let keyboardOpen = false;

      // Track when keyboard opens/closes by monitoring visualViewport
      if (window.visualViewport) {
          let initialHeight = window.visualViewport.height;

          window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', function() {
              const currentHeight = window.visualViewport.height;
              const heightDiff = initialHeight - currentHeight;

              // Keyboard is considered open if viewport height reduced by more than 150px
              if (heightDiff > 150 && !keyboardOpen) {
                  keyboardOpen = true;
                  scrollPosition = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
              }
              // Keyboard closed
              else if (heightDiff < 100 && keyboardOpen) {
                  keyboardOpen = false;

                  // Restore scroll position after a brief delay to ensure browser layout is complete
                  // iPhone Fix: DON'T force scroll on iPhone - Safari handles it naturally
                  if (!isIPhone) {
                      setTimeout(function() {
                          window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition);
                          document.documentElement.scrollTop = scrollPosition;
                          document.body.scrollTop = scrollPosition;
                      }, 100);
                  }
              }

              initialHeight = currentHeight;
          });
      }

      // Alternative: Monitor focus events on input fields inside iframe
      // (Backup method if visualViewport isn't supported)
      window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
          if (!window.visualViewport) {
              const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;
              const docHeight = document.documentElement.clientHeight;

              if (viewportHeight < docHeight * 0.7 && !keyboardOpen) {
                  keyboardOpen = true;
                  scrollPosition = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset;
              } else if (viewportHeight > docHeight * 0.85 && keyboardOpen) {
                  keyboardOpen = false;
                  // iPhone Fix: DON'T force scroll on iPhone - Safari handles it naturally
                  if (!isIPhone) {
                      setTimeout(function() {
                          window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition);
                      }, 100);
                  }
              }
          }
      });

      // Prevent body scroll when chat is maximized
      const iframe = document.querySelector('iframe.embeddedMessagingFrame');
      if (iframe) {
          const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
              mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
                  if (mutation.attributeName === 'class') {
                      const isMaximized = iframe.classList.contains('isMaximized') ||
                                         iframe.classList.contains('maximized');

                      if (isMaximized) {
                          // Chat is open - prevent body scroll
                          document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                          document.body.style.position = 'fixed';
                          document.body.style.width = '100%';
                          document.body.style.top = '-' + scrollPosition + 'px';
                      } else {
                          // Chat is minimized - restore scroll
                          document.body.style.overflow = '';
                          document.body.style.position = '';
                          document.body.style.width = '';
                          document.body.style.top = '';
                          window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition);
                      }
                  }
              });
          });

          observer.observe(iframe, { attributes: true });
      }

  })();
  </script>
  <!-- Mobile Keyboard Scroll Fix - END -->
  <!-- Standard Chatbot Code - END -->
<!-- Chat script End -->

function initFramework () {
	window.Aura = window.Aura || {};
	window.Aura.app = auraConfig["context"]["app"];
	window.Aura.beforeFrameworkInit = Aura.beforeFrameworkInit || [];
	window.Aura.beforeFrameworkInit.push(auraPreInitBlock);
	window.Aura.inlineJsReady = time();

	;

	if (!window.Aura.frameworkJsReady) {
		window.Aura.initConfig = auraConfig;
	} else {
		if (auraConfig.context) {
			if (auraConfig.context.dpbp) {
				// Set a data attribute on document.body to signal engine to bypass global patching
				document.body.setAttribute("data-global-patching-bypass", "temporary-bypass");
			}
		}

		// LockerService must be initialized before scripts can be executed.
		$A.lockerService.initialize(auraConfig.context);

		// scripts inside custom templates with Locker active are stored
		// until now since we need LockerService before running

		var scripts = window.Aura.inlineJsLocker;
		if (scripts) {
			for (var i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {
				$A.lockerService.runScript(scripts[i]["callback"], scripts[i]["namespace"]);
			}

			delete window.Aura.inlineJsLocker;
		}

		if (true) {
			$A.initAsync(auraConfig);
		} else if (false) {
			$A.initConfig(auraConfig);
		}
	}
	window.Aura.afterFrameworkInit = Aura.afterFrameworkInit || [];
	window.Aura.afterFrameworkInit.push(function auraPostInitBlock() {
        
    });
}
// Error msg
var x = document.getElementById("dismissError");
var errorReload = document.getElementById("auraErrorReload");
var auraErrorMask = document.getElementById("auraErrorMask");
var err = document.getElementById("auraError");
function closeErrorBox() {
	if (window["$A"]) {
		$A.util.removeClass(auraErrorMask, "auraForcedErrorBox");
		$A.util.removeClass($A.util.getElement("auraErrorReload"), "show");
	} else {
		document.body.removeChild(auraErrorMask);
	}
}
function squash(e) {
	e.preventDefault();
	e.stopPropagation();
}
if (x) {
	x.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
		closeErrorBox();
		squash(e);
	});
}

if (err) {
	err.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
		if (e.key === 'Escape') {
			squash(e);
			closeErrorBox();
		} else if (e.key === 'Tab') {
			// Focus trap
			squash(e);
			if (e.target === x && errorReload && errorReload.classList.contains('show')) {
				errorReload.focus();
			} else if (e.target === errorReload) {
				x.focus();
			}
		}
	});
}

if (auraErrorMask) {
	var errorMaskObserver = new MutationObserver(function(ms) {
		ms.forEach(function(mut) {
			if (mut.type === 'attributes' && mut.attributeName === 'class' && x && auraErrorMask.classList.contains('auraForcedErrorBox')) {
				requestAnimationFrame(function() { x.focus(); });
			}
		});
	});

	errorMaskObserver.observe(auraErrorMask, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
}

setTimeout(initFramework, 0); // ensure async



var appCssLoadingCount = 0;
function onLoadStyleSheets(event) {
	if(event){
		var element = event.target;
		element.removeEventListener("load",onLoadStyleSheets);
		element.removeEventListener("error",onLoadStyleSheetsError);
	}
	window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading = (--appCssLoadingCount) > 0;
	if (!window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading) {
		if (typeof window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback === "function") {
			window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback();
			window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback = undefined;
		}
	}
}
function onLoadStyleSheetsError(event) {
	window.Aura.bootstrap.hasCssLoadError = true;
	onLoadStyleSheets(event);
}

var auraCss = document.getElementsByClassName("auraCss"),
	current;
window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading = auraCss.length > 0;
for (var c=0,length=auraCss.length;c<length;c++) {
	current = auraCss[c];
	appCssLoadingCount++;
	current.addEventListener("load",onLoadStyleSheets);
	current.addEventListener("error",onLoadStyleSheetsError);
	current.href = current.getAttribute("data-href");
}
if (true) {
	setTimeout(function() {
		try {
			var s = document.getElementById('aura-preinit_to-remove');
			if (s) { s.remove(); }
		} catch(e) {}
	}, 0);
}
